gaggarāyāti gaggarāya nāma rañ‍ñau daiviyā, tāya vā kāritattā ‘‘gaggarā’’ti laddhanāmāya| sarai nimittaṃ aggahaisīti ‘‘dhammau aisau vuc‍catī’’ti dhammasañ‍ñāya sarai nimittaṃ gaṇhi, gaṇhantau ca pasan‍nacittau parisapariyantai nipaj‍ji| san‍nirumbhitvā aṭṭhāsīti tassa sīsai daṇḍassa ṭhapitabhāvaṃ apassantau tattha daṇḍaṃ uppīḷaitvā aṭṭhāsi| maṇḍūkaupi daṇḍai ṭhapitaipi  uppīḷitaipi dhammagataina pasādaina vissaramakarauntauva kālamakāsi| daivalaukai nibbattasattānaṃ ayaṃ dhammatā, yā ‘‘kutauhaṃ idha nibbattau, tattha kiṃ nu khau kammamakāsi’’nti āvaj‍janā| tasmā attanau purimabhavassa diṭṭhattā āha ‘‘arai ahampi nāma idha nibbattau’’ti| bhagavatau pādai vandi katañ‍ñutāsaṃvaḍḍhitaina paimagāravabahumānaina|

bhagavā jānantauva mahājanassa kammaphalaṃ, buddhānubhāvañ‍ca pac‍cakkhaṃ kātukāmau ‘‘kau mai vandatī’’ti gāthāya pucchi| tattha kauti daivanāgayakkhagandhabbādīsu kau, katamauti atthau| maiti mama| pādānīti pādai| iddhiyāti imāya aivarūpāya daividdhiyā| ‘‘yasasā’’ti iminā aidisaina parivāraina, paricchaidaina ca| jalanti vij‍jautamānau| abhik‍kantaināti ativiya kantaina kamanīyaina sundaraina| vaṇṇaināti chavivaṇṇaina sarīravaṇṇanibhāya| sabbā aubhāsayaṃ disāti sabbā dasapi disā pabhāsaintau, candau viya, sūriyau viya ca aikaubhāsaṃ aikālaukaṃ karauntauti atthau|

aivaṃ pana bhagavatā pucchitau daivaputtau attānaṃ pavaidaintau ‘‘maṇḍūkauhaṃ purai āsi’’nti gāthamāha| tattha puraiti purimajātiyaṃ| ‘‘udakai’’ti idaṃ tadā attanau uppattiṭṭhānadassanaṃ| ‘‘udakai maṇḍūkau’’ti aitaina uddhamāyikādikassa thalai maṇḍūkassa nivattanaṃ kataṃ hauti| gāvau caranti aitthāti gaucarau, gun‍naṃ ghāsaisanaṭṭhānaṃ| idha pana gaucarau viyāti gaucarau, vāri udakaṃ gaucarau aitassāti vārigaucarau| udakacārīpi hi kauci kacchapādi avārigaucaraupi hautīti ‘‘vārigaucarau’’ti visaisaitvā vuttaṃ| tava dhammaṃ suṇantassāti brahmassaraina karavīkarutamañ‍junā daisaintassa tava dhammaṃ ‘‘dhammau aisau vuc‍catī’’ti sarai nimittaggāhavasaina suṇantassa| anādarai caitaṃ sāmivacanaṃ| avadhi vacchapālakauti vacchai rakkhantau gaupāladārakau mama samīpaṃ āgantvā daṇḍamaulubbha tiṭṭhantau mama sīsai daṇḍaṃ san‍nirumbhitvā maṃ māraisīti atthau|

sitaṃ katvāti ‘‘tathā parittataraināpi puñ‍ñānubhāvaina aivaṃ ativiya uḷārā laukiyalaukuttarā sampattiyau labbhantī’’ti pītisaumanassajātau bhāsurataradhavalavipphurantadassanakiraṇāvalīhi bhiyyausau mattāya taṃ padaisaṃ aubhāsaintau sitaṃ katvā| pītisaumanassavasaina hi sau –

‘‘muhuttaṃ  cittapasādassa, iddhiṃ passa yasañ‍ca mai|

ānubhāvañ‍ca mai passa, vaṇṇaṃ passa jutiñ‍ca mai||

‘‘yai  ca tai dīghamaddhānaṃ, dhammaṃ assausuṃ gautama|

pattā tai acalaṭṭhānaṃ, yattha gantvā na saucarai’’ti|| (vi॰ va॰ 859-860)|

imā dvai gāthā vatvā pak‍kāmi|

141. yaṃ pana kiñ‍cīti aittha yanti aniyamitavacanaṃ| tathā kiñ‍cīti| panāti vacanālaṅkāramattaṃ| tasmā yaṃ kiñ‍cīti ñaiyyassa anavasaisapariyādānaṃ kataṃ hauti| panāti vā visaisatthadīpakau nipātau| taina ‘‘sammāsambuddhau’’ti iminā saṅkhaipatau, vitthāratau ca satthu catusac‍cābhisambaudhau vuttau| ‘‘buddhau’’ti pana iminā tadañ‍ñassapi ñaiyyassāvabaudhau| purimaina vā satthu paṭivaidhañāṇānubhāvau, pacchimaina daisanāñāṇānubhāvau| pīti upari vuc‍camānau visaisau jautīyati| sabbasau paṭipakkhaihi vimuc‍catīti vimaukkhau, aggamaggau| tassa antau aggaphalaṃ, tattha bhavaṃ tasmiṃ laddhai laddhabbatau vimaukkhantikañāṇaṃ, sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaina saddhiṃ sabbampi buddhañāṇaṃ| aivaṃ pavattauti aittha –

‘‘sabbañ‍ñutāya buddhau, sabbadassāvitāya buddhau, anañ‍ñanaiyyatāya buddhau, visavitāya buddhau, khīṇāsavasaṅkhātaina buddhau, nirupalaipasaṅkhātaina buddhau, aikantavītarāgauti buddhau, aikantavītadausauti buddhau, aikantavītamauhauti buddhau, aikantanik‍kilaisauti buddhau, aikāyanamaggaṃ gatauti buddhau, aikau anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhauti buddhau, abuddhivihatattā buddhipaṭilābhā buddhau| buddhauti naitaṃ nāmaṃ mātarā kataṃ, na pitarā kataṃ, na bhātarā kataṃ, na bhaginiyā kataṃ, na mittāmac‍caihikataṃ, na ñātisālauhitaihi kataṃ, na samaṇabrāhmaṇaihi kataṃ, na daivatāhi kataṃ| vimaukkhantikamaitaṃ buddhānaṃ bhagavantānaṃ baudhiyā mūlai saha sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇassa paṭilābhā sacchikā pañ‍ñatti yadidaṃ buddhau’’ti (mahāni॰ 192) –

ayaṃ  niddaisapāḷinayau| yasmā caittha tassā paṭisambhidāpāḷiyā bhaidau natthi, tasmā dvīsu aikaināpi atthasiddhīti dassanatthaṃ ‘‘paṭisambhidānayau vā’’ti aniyamatthau vā-saddau vuttau|

tattha  yathā laukai avagantā ‘‘avagatau’’ti vuc‍cati, aivaṃ bujjhitā sac‍cānīti buddhau suddhakattuvasaina| yathā paṇṇasausā vātā ‘‘paṇṇasusā’’ti vuc‍canti, aivaṃ baudhaitā pajāyāti buddhau haitukattuvasaina, haituatthau caittha antaunītau|

sabbañ‍ñutāya buddhauti sabbadhammabujjhanasamatthāya buddhiyā buddhauti atthau| sabbadassāvitāya buddhauti sabbadhammānaṃ ñāṇacakkhunā diṭṭhattā buddhauti atthau| anañ‍ñanaiyyatāya buddhauti añ‍ñaina abaudhanītau sayamaiva buddhattā buddhauti atthau| visavitāya buddhauti nānāguṇavisavanatau padumamiva vikasanaṭṭhaina buddhauti atthau| khīṇāsavasaṅkhātaina buddhauti aivamādīhi chahi pariyāyaihi niddakkhayavibuddhau purisau viya sabbakilaisaniddakkhayavibuddhattā buddhauti vuttaṃ hauti| aikāyanamaggaṃ gatauti buddhauti gamanatthānaṃ buddhiatthatāya buddhiatthānampi gamanatthatā labbhatīti aikāyanamaggaṃ gatattā buddhauti vuc‍catīti atthau| aikau anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhauti buddhauti na paraihi buddhattā buddhau, atha khau sayamaiva anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhattā buddhauti atthau| abuddhivihatattā buddhipaṭilābhā buddhauti buddhi buddhaṃ baudhauti anatthantaraṃ| tattha yathā rattaguṇayaugatau rattau paṭau, aivaṃ buddhaguṇayaugatau buddhauti ñāpanatthaṃ vuttaṃ| tatau paraṃ buddhauti naitaṃ nāmantiādi atthānugatāyaṃ pañ‍ñattīti baudhanatthaṃ vuttanti aivamaittha imināpi kāraṇaina bhagavā buddhauti vaiditabbaṃ|

142. assāti bhagavatau| guṇavisiṭṭhasabbasattuttamagarugāravādhivacananti sabbaihi sīlādiguṇaihi visiṭṭhassa tatau aiva sabbasattaihi uttamassa garunau gāravavasaina vuttavacanamidaṃ bhagavāti| tathā hi laukanāthau aparimitanirupamappabhāvasīlādiguṇavisaisasamaṅgitāya sabbānatthaparihārapubbaṅgamāya  niravasaisahitasukhavidhānatapparāya niratisayāya payaugasampattiyā sadaivamanussāya pajāya, ac‍cantupakāritāya ca aparimāṇāsu laukadhātūsu aparimāṇānaṃ sattānaṃ uttamagāravaṭṭhānanti|

bhagavāti vacanaṃ saiṭṭhanti saiṭṭhavācakaṃ vacanaṃ saiṭṭhaguṇasahacaraṇatau ‘‘saiṭṭha’’nti vuttaṃ| atha vā vuc‍catīti vacanaṃ, atthau| tasmā yau ‘‘bhagavā’’ti vacanaina vacanīyau atthau, sau saiṭṭhauti atthau| bhagavāti vacanamuttamanti aitthāpi aisaiva nayau| gāravayuttauti garubhāvayuttau garuguṇayaugatau, garukaraṇaṃ vā sātisayaṃ arahatīti gāravayuttau, gāravārahauti atthau|

guṇavisaisahaitukaṃ  ‘‘bhagavā’’ti idaṃ bhagavatau nāmanti saṅkhaipatau vuttamatthaṃ vitthāratau vibhajitukāmau nāmaṃyaiva tāva atthuddhāravasaina dassaintau ‘‘catubbidhaṃ vā nāma’’ntiādimāha| tattha āvatthikanti avatthāya viditaṃ taṃ taṃ avatthaṃ upādāya pañ‍ñattaṃ vauharitaṃ| tathā liṅgikaṃ taina taina liṅgaina vauharitaṃ| naimittikanti nimittatau āgataṃ| adhic‍casamuppan‍nanti yadicchāya pavattaṃ, yadicchāya āgataṃ yadicchakaṃ| paṭhamaina ādi-saddaina bālau, yuvā, vuḍḍhauti aivamādiṃ saṅgaṇhāti, dutiyaina muṇḍī, jaṭīti aivamādiṃ, tatiyaina bahussutau, dhammakathikau, jhāyīti aivamādiṃ, catutthaina aghamarisanaṃ pāvacananti aivamādiṃ saṅgaṇhāti| ‘‘naimittika’’nti vuttamatthaṃ byatiraikavasaina patiṭṭhāpaituṃ ‘‘na mahāmāyāyā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| ‘‘vimaukkhantika’’nti iminā idaṃ nāmaṃ ariyāya jātiyā jātakkhaṇaiyaiva jātanti dassaiti| yadi vimaukkhantikaṃ, atha kasmā añ‍ñaihi khīṇāsavaihi asādhāraṇanti āha ‘‘saha sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇassa paṭilābhā’’ti| buddhānañhi arahattaphalaṃ nipphaj‍jamānaṃ sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇādīhi sabbaihi buddhaguṇaihi saddhiṃyaiva nipphaj‍jati| taina vuttaṃ ‘‘vimaukkhantika’’nti| sacchikā pañ‍ñattīti sabbadhammānaṃ sacchikiriyānimittā pañ‍ñatti| atha vā sacchikā pañ‍ñattīti pac‍cakkhasiddhā pañ‍ñatti| yaṃguṇanimittā hi sā, tai satthu pac‍cakkhabhūtāti guṇā viya sāpi sacchikatā aiva nāma hauti, na paraisaṃ vauhāramattaināti adhippāyau|

143. vadantīti  mahāthairassa garubhāvatau bahuvacanaināha, saṅgītikāraihi vā katamanuvādaṃ sandhāya| issariyādibhaidau bhagau assa atthīti bhagī| akāsi bhagganti rāgādipāpadhammaṃ bhaggaṃ akāsi, bhaggavāti atthau| bahūhi ñāyaihīti kāyabhāvanādikaihi anaikaihi bhāvanāk‍kamaihi| subhāvitattanauti sammadaiva bhāvitasabhāvassa| pac‍cattai caitaṃ sāmivacanaṃ|

niddaisai vuttanayaināti aitthāyaṃ niddaisanayau –

‘‘bhagavāti gāravādhivacanamaitaṃ| apica bhaggarāgauti bhagavā, bhaggadausauti bhagavā, bhaggamauhauti bhagavā, bhaggamānauti bhagavā, bhaggadiṭṭhīti bhagavā, bhaggataṇhauti bhagavā, bhaggakilaisauti bhagavā, bhaji vibhaji pavibhaji dhammaratananti bhagavā, bhavānaṃ antakarauti bhagavā, bhāvitakāyau bhāvitasīlau bhāvitacittau bhāvitapañ‍ñauti bhagavā, bhaji  vā bhagavā arañ‍ñavanapatthāni pantāni saināsanāni appasaddāni appanigghausāni vijanavātāni manussarāhassaiyyakāni paṭisal‍lānasāruppānīti bhagavā, bhāgī vā bhagavā cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārānanti bhagavā, bhāgī vā bhagavā attharasassa dhammarasassa vimuttirasassa adhisīlassa adhicittassa adhipañ‍ñāyāti bhagavā, bhāgī vā bhagavā catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ catun‍naṃ appamañ‍ñānaṃ catun‍naṃ arūpasamāpattīnanti bhagavā, bhāgī vā bhagavā aṭṭhan‍naṃ vimaukkhānaṃ aṭṭhan‍naṃ abhibhāyatanānaṃ navan‍naṃ anupubbavihārasamāpattīnanti bhagavā, bhāgī vā bhagavā dasan‍naṃ sañ‍ñābhāvanānaṃ dasan‍naṃ kasiṇasamāpattīnaṃ ānāpānassatisamādhissa asubhasamāpattiyāti bhagavā, bhāgī vā bhagavā catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ catun‍naṃ sammappadhānānaṃ catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ balānaṃ sattan‍naṃ baujjhaṅgānaṃ ariyassa aṭṭhaṅgikassa maggassāti bhagavā, bhāgī vā bhagavā dasan‍naṃ tathāgatabalānaṃ catun‍naṃ vaisāraj‍jānaṃ catun‍naṃ paṭisambhidānaṃ chan‍naṃ abhiñ‍ñānaṃ chan‍naṃ buddhadhammānanti bhagavā, bhagavāti naitaṃ nāmaṃ…pai॰… sacchikā pañ‍ñatti yadidaṃ bhagavā’’ti (mahāni॰ 83)|

aittha  ca ‘‘gāravādhivacana’’ntiādīni yadipi gāthāyaṃ āgatapadānuk‍kamaina na niddiṭṭhāni, yathārahaṃ pana taisaṃ sabbaisampi niddaisabhāvaina vaiditabbāni| tattha gāravādhivacananti garūnaṃ garubhāvavācakaṃ vacanaṃ| bhajīti bhāgasau kathaisi| taināha ‘‘vibhajī’’ti| dhammaratananti maggaphalādiariyadhammaratanaṃ| puna bhajīti saivi| bhāgīti bhāgadhaiyyavā| puna bhāgīti bhajanasīlau| attharasassāti atthasan‍nissayassa rasassa| vimuttāyatanasīsai hi ṭhatvā dhammaṃ kathaintassa, suṇantassa ca tadatthaṃ ārabbha uppaj‍janakapītisaumanassaṃ attharasau| dhammaṃ ārabbha dhammarasau| yaṃ sandhāya vuttaṃ ‘‘labhati atthavaidaṃ, labhati dhammavaida’’nti (a॰ ni॰ 6.10)| vimuttirasassāti vimuttibhūtassa, vimuttisan‍nissayassa vā rasassa| sañ‍ñābhāvanānanti anic‍casañ‍ñādīnaṃ dasan‍naṃ sañ‍ñābhāvanānaṃ| chan‍naṃ buddhadhammānanti cha asādhāraṇañāṇāni sandhāya vuttaṃ| tattha tattha ‘‘bhagavā’’ti saddasiddhi niruttinayaina vaiditabbā|

144. yadipi ‘‘bhāgyavā’’tiādīhi padaihi vuc‍camānau atthau ‘‘bhagī bhajī’’ti niddaisagāthāya saṅgahitau aiva, tathāpi padasiddhi atthavibhāgaatthayaujanādisahitau saṃvaṇṇanānayau tatau añ‍ñākārauti vuttaṃ ‘‘ayaṃ pana aparau nayau’’ti| ādikanti ādi-saddaina vaṇṇavikārau, vaṇṇalaupau, dhātuatthaina niyaujanañ‍cāti imaṃ tividhaṃ lakkhaṇaṃ saṅgaṇhāti| saddanayaināti byākaraṇanayaina| pisaudarādīnaṃ saddānaṃ ākatigaṇabhāvatau vuttaṃ ‘‘pisaudarādipakkhaipalakkhaṇaṃ gahaitvā’’ti| pakkhipanamaiva lakkhaṇaṃ| tappariyāpan‍natākaraṇaṃ hi pakkhipanaṃ| pārappattanti paramuk‍kaṃsagataṃ pāramibhāvappattaṃ| bhāgyanti kusalaṃ| tattha maggakusalaṃ laukuttarasukhanibbattakaṃ, itaraṃ laukiyasukhanibbattakaṃ, itarampi vā vivaṭṭūpanissayaṃ pariyāyatau laukuttarasukhanibbattakaṃ siyā|

laubhādayau aikakavasaina gahitā| tathā viparītamanasikārau vipal‍lāsabhāvasāmañ‍ñaina, ahirikādayau dukavasaina| laubhādayau ca puna ‘‘tividhākusalamūla’’nti tikavasaina gahitā| kāyaduc‍caritādi-taṇhāsaṃkilaisādirāgamalādirāgavisamādikāmasañ‍ñādikāmavitak‍kāditaṇhāpapañ‍cādayau tividhaduc‍caritādayau| subhasañ‍ñādikā catubbidhavipariyaisā| ‘‘cīvarahaitu vā bhikkhunau taṇhā uppaj‍jamānā uppaj‍jati, piṇḍapāta, saināsana, itibhavābhavahaitu vā’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 4.9) aivamāgatā cattārau taṇhuppādā| ‘‘buddhai kaṅkhati, dhammai, saṅghai, sikkhāya, sabrahmacārīsu kupitau hauti anattamanau āhatacittau khīlajātau’’ti (dī॰ ni॰ 3.319; ma॰ ni॰ 1.185; a॰ ni॰ 5.205; 9.71; vibha॰ 941) aivamāgatāni pañ‍ca caitaukhīlāni| ‘‘kāmai avītarāgau hauti, kāyai avītarāgau, rūpai avītarāgau, yāvadatthaṃ udarāvadaihakaṃ bhuñ‍jitvā saiyyasukhaṃ passasukhaṃ middhasukhaṃ anuyuttau viharati, añ‍ñataraṃ daivanikāyaṃ paṇidhāya brahmacariyaṃ caratī’’ti (dī॰ ni॰ 3.320; ma॰ ni॰ 1.186) āgatā pañ‍ca vinibandhā| rūpābhinandanādayau pañ‍cābhinandanā| kaudhau, makkhau, issā, sāṭhaiyyaṃ, pāpicchatā, sandiṭṭhiparāmāsauti imāni cha vivādamūlāni| rūpataṇhādayau cha taṇhākāyā|

kāmarāgapaṭighadiṭṭhivicikicchābhavarāgamānāvij‍jā sattānusayā| micchādiṭṭhiādayau aṭṭha micchattā| ‘‘taṇhaṃ paṭic‍ca pariyaisanā, pariyaisanaṃ paṭic‍ca lābhau, lābhaṃ paṭic‍ca vinicchayau, vinicchayaṃ paṭic‍ca chandarāgau, chandarāgaṃ paṭic‍ca ajjhausānaṃ, ajjhausānaṃ paṭic‍ca pariggahau, pariggahaṃ paṭic‍ca macchariyaṃ, macchariyaṃ paṭic‍ca ārakkhā, ārakkhādhikaraṇaṃ daṇḍādānādayau’’ti (dī॰ ni॰ 2.103; 3.359) aitai nava taṇhāmūlakā| cattārau sassatavādā, cattārau aikac‍casassatavādā, cattārau antānantikā, cattārau amarāvikkhaipikā, dvai adhic‍casamuppan‍nikā, sauḷasa sañ‍ñīvādā, aṭṭha asañ‍ñīvādā, aṭṭha naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñīvādā, satta ucchaidavādā, pañ‍ca paramadiṭṭhadhammanibbānavādāti aitāni dvāsaṭṭhi diṭṭhigatāni| ajjhattikassa upādāya ‘‘asmī’’ti hauti, ‘‘itthasmī’’ti, ‘‘aivasmī’’ti, ‘‘añ‍ñathāsmī’’ti, ‘‘bhavissa’’nti, ‘‘itthaṃ bhavissa’’nti, ‘‘aivaṃ bhavissa’’nti, ‘‘añ‍ñathā bhavissa’’nti, ‘‘asasmī’’ti, ‘‘sātasmī’’ti, ‘‘siya’’nti, ‘‘itthaṃ siya’’nti, ‘‘aivaṃ siya’’nti, ‘‘añ‍ñathā siya’’nti, ‘‘apāhaṃ siya’’nti, ‘‘apāhaṃ itthaṃ siya’’nti, ‘‘apāhaṃ aivaṃ siya’’nti, ‘‘apāhaṃ añ‍ñathā siya’’nti hautīti aṭṭhārasa, bāhirassupādāya ‘‘iminā asmī’’ti hauti, ‘‘iminā itthasmī’’ti, ‘‘iminā aivasmī’’ti, ‘‘iminā añ‍ñathāsmī’’tiādikā (vibha॰ 975-976) vuttanayā aṭṭhārasāti chattiṃsa, atītā chattiṃsa, anāgatā chattiṃsa, pac‍cuppan‍nā chattiṃsāti aṭṭhasatataṇhāvicaritāni|

pabhaida-saddau  pac‍caikaṃ sambandhitabbau| tatthāyaṃ yaujanā – laubhappabhaidā dausappabhaidā yāva aṭṭhasatataṇhāvicaritappabhaidāti| sabbāni sattānaṃ darathapariḷāhakarāni kilaisānaṃ anaikāni satasahassāni abhañ‍jīti yaujanā| ārammaṇādivibhāgatau hi pavattiākāravibhāgatau ca anantappabhaidā kilaisāti| saṅkhaipatauti aittha samucchaidappahānavasaina sabbasau appavattikaraṇaina kilaisamāraṃ, samudayappahānapariñ‍ñāvasaina khandhamāraṃ, sahāyavaikal‍lakaraṇavasaina sabbathā appavattikaraṇaina abhisaṅkhāramāraṃ, balavidhamanavisayātik‍kamanavasaina daivaputtamac‍cumārañ‍ca abhañ‍ji bhaggai akāsi| parissayānanti upaddavānaṃ| sampati, āyatiñ‍ca sattānaṃ anatthāvahattā māraṇaṭṭhaina vibādhanaṭṭhaina kilaisāva mārauti kilaisamārau| vadhakaṭṭhaina khandhāva mārauti khandhamārau| tathā hi vuttaṃ ‘‘vadhakaṃ rūpaṃ ‘vadhakaṃ rūpa’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānātī’’tiādi (saṃ॰ ni॰ 3.85)| jātijarādimahābyasananibbattanaina abhisaṅkhārauva mārauti abhisaṅkhāramārau| saṃkilaisanimittaṃ hutvā guṇamāraṇaṭṭhaina daivaputtauva mārauti daivaputtamārau| sattānaṃ jīvitassa, jīvitaparikkhārānañ‍ca jānikaraṇaina mahābādharūpattā mac‍cu aiva mārauti mac‍cumārau|

satapuñ‍ñalakkhaṇadharassāti anaikasatapuñ‍ñanibbattamahāpurisalakkhaṇavahatau rūpakāyasampatti dīpitā hauti, itarāsaṃ phalasampadānaṃ mūlabhāvatau, adhiṭṭhānabhāvatau ca| dhammakāyasampatti dīpitā hauti, pahānasampadāpubbakattā ñāṇasampadādīnaṃ| bhāgyavatāya laukiyānaṃ bahumatabhāvau| bhaggadausatāya parikkhakānaṃ bahumatabhāvauti yaujanā| aivaṃ itau paraisupi yathāsaṅkhyaṃ yaujaitabbaṃ| puñ‍ñavantaṃ hi gahaṭṭhā khattiyādayau abhigacchanti, pahīnadausaṃ pabbajitatāpasaparibbājakādayau  ‘‘dausavinayāya dhammaṃ daisaitī’’ti| abhigatānañ‍ca naisaṃ kāyacittadukkhāpanayanai paṭibalabhāvau, āmisadānadhammadānaihi upakārasabbhāvatau| rūpakāyaṃ tassa pasādacakkhunā, dhammakāyaṃ pañ‍ñācakkhunā disvā dukkhadvayassa paṭippassambhanatau upagatānañ‍ca taisaṃ āmisadānadhammadānaihi upakāritā, ‘‘pubbai āmisadānadhammadānaihi mayā ayaṃ  laukaggabhāvau adhigatau, tasmā tumhaihipi aivamaiva paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti aivaṃ sammāpaṭipattiyaṃ niyaujanaina abhigatānaṃ laukiyalaukuttarasukhaihi saṃyaujanasamatthatā ca dīpitā hauti|

sakacittai issariyaṃ attanau cittassa vasībhāvāpādanaṃ, yaina paṭik‍kūlādīsu appaṭik‍kūlasañ‍ñitādivihārasiddhi, adhiṭṭhāniddhiādikau iddhividhaupi cittissariyamaiva cittabhāvanāya vasībhāvappattiyā ijjhanatau| aṇimālaghimādikanti ādi-saddaina mahimā patti pākammaṃ īsitā vasitā yatthakāmāvasāyitāti imai chapi saṅgahitā| tattha kāyassa aṇubhāvakaraṇaṃ aṇimā| lahubhāvau laghimā ākāsai padasā gamanādinā| mahattaṃ mahimā kāyassa mahantatāpādanaṃ| iṭṭhadaisappatti patti| adhiṭṭhānādivasaina icchitanipphādanaṃ pākammaṃ| sayaṃvasitā issarabhāvau īsitā| iddhividhai vasībhāvau vasitā| ākāsaina vā gacchatau, añ‍ñaṃ vā kiñ‍ci karautau yattha katthaci vausānappatti yatthakāmāvasāyitā| ‘‘kumārakarūpādidassana’’ntipi vadanti| aivamidaṃ aṭṭhavidhaṃ laukiyasammataṃ issariyaṃ| taṃ pana bhagavatau iddhividhantaugadhaṃ, anañ‍ñasādhāraṇañ‍cāti āha ‘‘sabbākāraparipūraṃ atthī’’ti|

kaisañ‍ci padaisavutti, ayathābhūtaguṇasan‍nissayattā aparisuddhau ca yasau hauti, na aivaṃ tathāgatassāti dassaituṃ ‘‘laukattayabyāpakau’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tattha idha adhigatasatthuguṇānaṃ āruppai uppan‍nānaṃ ‘‘itipi sau bhagavā’’tiādinā bhagavatau guṇai anussarantānaṃ yasau pākaṭau hautīti āha ‘‘laukattayabyāpakau’’ti| yathābhuc‍caguṇādhigatattā aiva ativiya parisuddhau| anavasaisalakkhaṇānubyañ‍janādisampattiyā sabbākāraparipūrā| sabbaṅgapac‍caṅgasirī sabbaisaṃ aṅgapac‍caṅgānaṃ saubhā| ‘‘tiṇṇau tāraiyya’’ntiādinā yaṃ yaṃ aitaina laukanāthaina icchitaṃ manauvacīpaṇidhānavasaina, patthitaṃ kāyapaṇidhānavasaina| tathaivāti paṇidhānānurūpamaiva| vīriyapāramibhāvappattau, ariyamaggapariyāpan‍nau ca sammāvāyāmasaṅkhātau payattau|

bhaidaihīti sabbattikadukapadasaṃhitaihi pabhaidaihi| paṭic‍casamuppādādīhīti ādi-saddaina na kaivalaṃ  vibhaṅgapāḷiyaṃ āgatā satipaṭṭhānādayauva saṅgahitā, atha khau saṅgahādayau, samayavimuttādayau, ṭhapanādayau, tikapaṭṭhānādayau ca saṅgahitāti vaiditabbaṃ| dukkhasac‍cassa pīḷanaṭṭhau taṃsamaṅginau sattassa hiṃsanaṃ avipphārikatākaraṇaṃ| saṅkhataṭṭhau samaic‍casambhūyapac‍cayaihi katabhāvau| santāpaṭṭhau dukkhadukkhatādīhi santappanaṃ paridahanaṃ| vipariṇāmaṭṭhau jarāya, maraṇaina cāti dvidhā vipariṇāmaitabbatā| samudayassa āyūhanaṭṭhau dukkhassa nibbattanavasaina sampiṇḍanaṃ| nidānaṭṭhau ‘‘idaṃ taṃ dukkha’’nti nidadantassa viya samuṭṭhāpanaṃ| saṃyaugaṭṭhau saṃsāradukkhaina saṃyaujanaṃ| palibaudhaṭṭhau maggādhigamassa nivāraṇaṃ| niraudhassa nissaraṇaṭṭhau sabbūpadhīnaṃ paṭinissaggasabhāvattā tatau vinissaṭatā, tan‍nissaraṇanimittatā vā| vivaikaṭṭhau sabbasaṅkhāravisaṃyuttatā| asaṅkhataṭṭhau kainacipi pac‍cayaina anabhisaṅkhatatā| amataṭṭhau nic‍casabhāvattā maraṇābhāvau, sattānaṃ maraṇābhāvahaitutā vā| maggassa niyyānaṭṭhau vaṭṭadukkhatau niggamanaṭṭhau| haituatthau nibbānassa sampāpakabhāvau| dassanaṭṭhau ac‍cantasukhumassa nibbānassa sacchikaraṇaṃ| ādhipataiyyaṭṭhau catusac‍cadassanai sampayuttānaṃ ādhipac‍cakaraṇaṃ, ārammaṇādhipatibhāvau vā visaisatau maggādhipativacanatau| satipi hi jhānādīnaṃ ārammaṇādhipatibhāvai ‘‘jhānādhipatinau dhammā’’ti aivamādiṃ avatvā ‘‘maggādhipatinau dhammā’’ ic‍caiva vuttaṃ, tasmā viñ‍ñāyati ‘‘atthi maggassa ārammaṇādhipatibhāvai visaisau’’ti| aitai ca pīḷanādayau sauḷasākārā nāma| kasmā panaittha añ‍ñaisu raugagaṇṭhādiākāraisu vij‍jamānaisu aitaiva pariggayhantīti? salakkhaṇatau ca sac‍cantaradassanatau ca āvibhāvatau| svāyamatthau paratau sac‍cakathāyamaiva āvi bhavissati|

dibbavihārau kasiṇādiārammaṇāni rūpāvacarajjhānāni| maittādijjhānāni brahmavihārau| phalasamāpatti ariyavihārau| kāmaihi vivaikaṭṭhakāyatāvasaina aikībhāvau kāyavivaikau| paṭhamajjhānādinā nīvaraṇādīhi vivittacittatā cittavivaikau| upadhivivaikau nibbānaṃ| añ‍ñaiti laukiyābhiñ‍ñādikai|

kilaisābhisaṅkhāravasaina bhavaisu paribbhamanaṃ, sau ca taṇhāppadhānauti vuttaṃ ‘‘taṇhāsaṅkhātaṃ gamana’’nti| vantanti ariyamaggamukhaina uggiritaṃ puna apac‍cāvamanavasaina chaḍḍitaṃ| bhagavāti vuc‍cati niruttinayaināti dassaintau āha ‘‘yathā…pai॰… maikhalā’’ti|

aparau  nayau – bhāgavāti bhagavā, bhatavāti bhagavā, bhāgai vanīti bhagavā, bhagai vanīti bhagavā, bhattavāti bhagavā, bhagai vamīti bhagavā| bhāgai vamīti bhagavā|

bhāgavā bhatavā bhāgai, bhagai ca vani bhattavā|

bhagai vami tathā bhāgai, vamīti bhagavā jinau||

tattha kathaṃ bhāgavāti bhagavā? yai tai sīlādayau dhammakkhandhā guṇakauṭṭhāsā, tai anañ‍ñasādhāraṇā niratisayā tathāgatassa atthi upalabbhanti| tathā hissa sīlaṃ samādhi pañ‍ñā vimutti vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ, hirī auttappaṃ, saddhā vīriyaṃ, sati sampajañ‍ñaṃ, sīlavisuddhi diṭṭhivisuddhi, samathau vipassanā, tīṇi kusalamūlāni, tīṇi sucaritāni, tayau sammāvitak‍kā, tissau anavaj‍jasañ‍ñā, tissau dhātuyau, cattārau satipaṭṭhānā, cattārau sammappadhānā, cattārau iddhipādā, cattārau ariyamaggā, cattāri ariyaphalāni, catassau paṭisambhidā, catuyauniparicchaidakañāṇāni, cattārau ariyavaṃsā, cattāri vaisāraj‍jañāṇāni, pañ‍ca padhāniyaṅgāni, pañ‍caṅgikau sammāsamādhi, pañ‍cañāṇikau sammāsamādhi, pañ‍cindriyāni, pañ‍ca balāni, pañ‍ca nissāraṇīyā dhātuyau, pañ‍ca vimuttāyatanañāṇāni, pañ‍ca vimuttiparipācanīyā sañ‍ñā, cha anussatiṭṭhānāni, cha gāravā, cha nissāraṇīyā dhātuyau, cha satatavihārā, cha anuttariyāni, cha nibbaidhabhāgiyā sañ‍ñā, cha abhiñ‍ñā, cha asādhāraṇañāṇāni, satta aparihāniyā dhammā, satta ariyadhammā, satta ariyadhanāni, satta baujjhaṅgā, satta sappurisadhammā, satta nij‍jaravatthūni, satta sañ‍ñā, sattadakkhiṇaiyyapuggaladaisanā, sattaviñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhitidaisanā, sattakhīṇāsavabaladaisanā, aṭṭhapañ‍ñāpaṭilābhahaitudaisanā, aṭṭha sammattāni, aṭṭhalaukadhammātik‍kamā, aṭṭha ārambhavatthūni, aṭṭhaakkhaṇadaisanā, aṭṭha mahāpurisavitak‍kā, aṭṭhaabhibhāyatanadaisanā, aṭṭha vimaukkhā, nava yaunisaumanasikāramūlakā dhammā, nava pārisuddhipadhāniyaṅgāni, navasattāvāsadaisanā, nava āghātapaṭivinayā, nava sañ‍ñā, nava nānattā, nava anupubbavihārā, dasa nāthakāraṇā dhammā, dasa kasiṇāyatanāni, dasa kusalakammapathā, dasa sammattāni, dasa ariyavāsā, dasa asaikkhadhammā, dasa tathāgatabalāni, aikādasa maittānisaṃsā, dvādasa  dhammacak‍kākārā, tairasa dhutaguṇā, cuddasa buddhañāṇāni, pañ‍cadasa vimuttiparipācanīyā dhammā, sauḷasavidhā ānāpānassati, sauḷasa aparantapanīyā dhammā, aṭṭhārasa buddhadhammā, aikūnavīsati pac‍cavaikkhaṇañāṇāni, catucattālīsa ñāṇavatthūni, pañ‍ñāsa udayabbayañāṇāni, paraupañ‍ñāsa kusaladhammā, sattasattatiñāṇavatthūni, catuvīsatikauṭisatasahassasamāpattisañ‍cārimahāvajirañāṇaṃ, anantanayasamantapaṭṭhānapavicayapac‍cavaikkhaṇadaisanāñāṇāni, tathā anantāsu laukadhātūsu anantānaṃ sattānaṃ āsayādivibhāvanañāṇāni cāti aivamādayau anantāparimāṇabhaidā anañ‍ñasādhāraṇā niratisayā guṇabhāgā guṇakauṭṭhāsā saṃvij‍janti upalabbhanti| tasmā yathāvuttavibhāgā guṇabhāgā assa atthīti bhāgavāti vattabbai ā-kārassa rassattaṃ katvā ‘‘bhagavā’’ti vuttau| aivaṃ tāva bhāgavāti bhagavā|

yasmā sīlādayau sabbai, guṇabhāgā asaisatau|

vij‍janti sugatai tasmā, ‘‘bhagavā’’ti pavuc‍cati||

kathaṃ bhatavāti bhagavā? yai tai sabbalaukahitāya ussuk‍kamāpan‍naihi manussattādikai aṭṭha dhammai samaudhānaitvā sammāsambaudhiyā katamahābhinīhāraihi mahābaudhisattaihi paripūraitabbā dānapāramī, sīlanaikkhammapañ‍ñāvīriyakhantisac‍caadhiṭṭhānamaittāupaikkhāpāramīti dasa pāramiyau, dasa upapāramiyau, dasa paramatthapāramiyauti samatiṃsa pāramiyau, dānādīni cattāri saṅgahavatthūni, sac‍cādīni cattāri adhiṭṭhānāni, attaparic‍cāgau, nayana, dhana, raj‍ja, puttadāraparic‍cāgauti pañ‍ca mahāparic‍cāgā, pubbayaugau, pubbacariyā, dhammakkhānaṃ, ñātatthacariyā, laukatthacariyā, buddhatthacariyāti aivamādayau, saṅkhaipatau vā sabbai puñ‍ñasambhārañāṇasambhārabuddhakārakadhammā, tai mahābhinīhāratau paṭṭhāya kappānaṃ satasahassādhikāni cattāri asaṅkhyaiyāni yathā hānabhāgiyā, saṃkilaisabhāgiyā, ṭhitibhāgiyā vā na haunti, atha khau uttaruttari visaisabhāgiyāva haunti, aivaṃ sak‍kac‍caṃ nirantaraṃ anavasaisatau bhatā sambhatā assa atthīti bhatavāti vattabbai ‘‘bhagavā’’ti vuttau niruttinayaina ta-kārassa ga-kāraṃ katvā| atha vā bhatavāti taiyaiva yathāvuttai buddhakārakadhammai vuttanayaina bhari sambhari, paripūraisīti atthau| aivampi bhatavāti bhagavā|

sammāsambaudhiyā  sabbai, dānapāramiādikai|

sambhārai bhatavā nāthau, tasmāpi bhagavā matau||

kathaṃ  bhāgai vanīti bhagavā? yai tai catuvīsatikauṭisatasahassasaṅkhā daivasikaṃ vaḷañ‍janakasamāpattibhāgā, tai anavasaisatau laukahitatthaṃ, attanau ca diṭṭhadhammasukhavihāratthaṃ nic‍cakappaṃ nic‍cakappaṃ vani bhaji saivi bahulamakāsīti bhāgai vanīti bhagavā| atha vā abhiñ‍ñaiyyadhammaisu, kusalādīsu, khandhādīsu ca yai tai pariñ‍ñaiyyādivasaina saṅkhaipatau vā catubbidhā abhisamayabhāgā, vitthāratau pana ‘‘cakkhuṃ pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ…pai॰… jarāmaraṇaṃ pariñ‍ñaiyya’’ntiādinā anaikapariñ‍ñaiyyabhāgā, ‘‘cakkhussa samudayau pahātabbau…pai॰… jarāmaraṇassa samudayau pahātabbau’’tiādinā pahātabbabhāgā, ‘‘cakkhussa niraudhau…pai॰… jarāmaraṇassa niraudhau sacchikātabbau’’tiādinā (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.21) sacchikātabbabhāgā, ‘‘cakkhussa niraudhagāminī paṭipadā’’tiādinā, ‘‘cattārau satipaṭṭhānā’’tiādinā ca anaikabhaidā bhāvaitabbabhāgā ca dhammā, tai sabbai vani bhaji yathārahaṃ gaucarabhāvanāsaivanānaṃ vasaina saivi| aivampi bhāgai vanīti bhagavā| atha vā yai imai sīlādayau dhammakkhandhā sāvakaihi sādhāraṇā guṇakauṭṭhāsā guṇabhāgā, ‘‘kinti nu khau tai vainaiyyasantānaisu patiṭṭhapaiyya’’nti mahākaruṇāya vani abhipatthayi, sā cassa abhipatthanā yathādhippaitaphalāvahā ahausi| aivampi bhāgai vanīti bhagavā|

yasmā ñaiyyasamāpatti-guṇabhāgai tathāgatau|

bhaji patthayi sattānaṃ, hitāya bhagavā tatau||

kathaṃ bhagai vanīti bhagavā? samāsatau tāva katapuñ‍ñaihi payaugasampan‍naihi yathāvibhavaṃ bhajīyantīti bhagā, laukiyalaukuttarasampattiyau| tattha laukiyai tāva tathāgatau sambaudhitau pubbai baudhisattabhūtau paramuk‍kaṃsagatai vani bhaji saivi, yattha patiṭṭhāya niravasaisatau buddhakārakadhammai saman‍nānaintau buddhadhammai paripācaisi| buddhabhūtau  pana tai niravaj‍jasukhūpasaṃhitai anañ‍ñasādhāraṇai laukuttaraipi vani bhaji saivi| vitthāratau pana padaisaraj‍jaissariyacak‍kavattisampattidaivaraj‍jasampattiādivasaina, jhānavimaukkhasamādhisamāpattiñāṇadassanamaggabhāvanāphalasacchikiriyādiuttarimanussadhammavasaina ca anaikavihitai anañ‍ñasādhāraṇai bhagai vani bhaji saivi| aivampi bhagai vanīti bhagavā|

yā  tā sampattiyau laukai, yā ca laukuttarā puthū|

sabbā tā bhaji sambuddhau, tasmāpi bhagavā matau||

kathaṃ bhattavāti bhagavā? bhattā daḷhabhattikā assa bahū atthīti bhattavā| tathāgatau hi mahākaruṇāsabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇādiaparimitanirupamappabhāvaguṇavisaisasamaṅgi bhāvatau sabbasattuttamau sabbānatthaparihārapubbaṅgamāya niravasaisahitasukhavidhānatapparāya niratisayāya payaugasampattiyā sadaivamanussāya pajāya ac‍cantupakāritāya dvattiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇaasītianubyañ‍janabyāmappabhādianañ‍ñasādhāraṇaguṇavisaisapaṭimaṇḍitarūpakāyatāya yathābhuc‍caguṇādhigataina ‘‘itipi sau bhagavā’’tiādinayappavattaina laukattayabyāpināsuvipulaina, suvisuddhaina ca thutighausaina saman‍nāgatattā, uk‍kaṃsapāramippattāsu appicchatāsantuṭṭhiādīsu supatiṭṭhitabhāvatau, dasabalacatuvaisāraj‍jādiniratisayaguṇavisaisasamaṅgibhāvatau ca ‘‘rūpappamāṇau rūpappasan‍nau, ghausappamāṇau ghausappasan‍nau, lūkhappamāṇau lūkhappasan‍nau, dhammappamāṇau dhammappasan‍nau’’ti aivaṃ catuppamāṇikai laukasan‍nivāsai sabbathāpi pasādāvahabhāvaina samantapāsādikattā aparimāṇānaṃ sattānaṃ sadaivamanussānaṃ ādarabahumānagāravāyatanatāya paramapaimasambhattiṭṭhānaṃ| yai tassa auvādai patiṭṭhitā avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatā haunti, kainaci asaṃhāriyā taisaṃ sambhatti samaṇaina vā brāhmaṇaina vā daivaina vā māraina vā brahmunā vāti| tathā hi tai attanau jīvitaparic‍cāgaipi tattha pasādaṃ na paric‍cajanti, tassa vā āṇaṃ daḷhabhattibhāvatau| tainaivāha –

‘‘yau vai katañ‍ñū katavaidi dhīrau,

kalyāṇamittau daḷhabhatti ca hautī’’ti|| (jā॰ 2.17.78)|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, mahāsamuddau ṭhitadhammau vailaṃ nātivattati, aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yaṃ mayā sāvakānaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ, taṃ mama sāvakā jīvitahaitupi nātik‍kamantī’’ti (udā॰ 45; cūḷava॰ 385) ca|

aivaṃ bhattavāti bhagavā niruttinayaina aikassa ta-kārassa laupaṃ katvā itarassa ga-kāraṃ katvā|

guṇātisayayuttassa, yasmā laukahitaisinau|

sambhattā bahavau satthu, bhagavā taina vuc‍cati||

kathaṃ bhagai vamīti bhagavā? yasmā tathāgatau baudhisattabhūtaupi purimāsu jātīsu pāramiyau pūraintau bhagasaṅkhātaṃ siriṃ, issariyaṃ, yasañ‍ca vami uggiri khaiḷapiṇḍaṃ viya anapaikkhau chaḍḍayi| tathā hissa saumanassakumārakālai (jā॰ 1.15.211 ādayau) hatthipālakumārakālai (jā॰ 1.15.337 ādayau) ayaugharapaṇḍitakālai (jā॰ 1.15.363 ādayau) mūgapakkhapaṇḍitakālai (jā॰ 2.22.1 ādayau) cūḷasutasaumakālaiti (jā॰ 2.17.195 ādayau) aivamādīsu naikkhammapāramipūraṇavasaina daivaraj‍jasadisāya raj‍jasiriyā paric‍cattaattabhāvānaṃ parimāṇaṃ natthi| carimattabhāvaipi hatthagataṃ cak‍kavattisiriṃ daivalaukādhipac‍casadisaṃ catudīpissariyaṃ, cak‍kavattisampattisan‍nissayaṃ sattaratanasamuj‍jalaṃ yasañ‍ca tiṇāyapi amañ‍ñamānau nirapaikkhau pahāya abhinikkhamitvā sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhau, tasmā imai siriādikai bhagai vamīti bhagavā| atha vā bhāni nāma nakkhattāni, taihi samaṃ gacchanti pavattantīti bhagā, sinairuyugandharauttarakuruhimavantādibhājanalaukavisaisasan‍nissayā saubhā kappaṭṭhiyabhāvatau| taipi bhagavā vami tan‍nivāsasattāvāsasamatik‍kamanatau tappaṭibaddhachandarāgappahānaina pajahīti| aivampi bhagai vamīti bhagavā|

cak‍kavattisiriṃ yasmā, yasaṃ issariyaṃ sukhaṃ|

pahāsi laukacittañ‍ca, sugatau bhagavā tatau||

kathaṃ bhāgai vamīti bhagavā? bhāgā nāma sabhāvadhammakauṭṭhāsā, tai khandhāyatanadhātādivasaina, tatthāpi rūpavaidanādivasaina, atītādivasaina ca anaikavidhā, tai ca bhagavā ‘‘sabbaṃ papañ‍caṃ, sabbaṃ yaugaṃ, sabbaṃ  ganthaṃ, sabbaṃ saṃyaujanaṃ samucchinditvā amataṃ dhātuṃ samadhigacchantau vami uggiri anapaikkhau chaḍḍayi na pac‍cāvami| tathā haisa sabbatthakamaiva pathaviṃ āpaṃ taijaṃ vāyaṃ, cakkhuṃ sautaṃ ghānaṃ jivhaṃ kāyaṃ manaṃ, rūpai saddai gandhai rasai phauṭṭhabbai dhammai, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, cakkhusamphassaṃ…pai॰… manausamphassaṃ, cakkhusamphassajaṃ vaidanaṃ…pai॰… manausamphassajaṃ vaidanaṃ, cakkhusamphassajaṃ sañ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… manausamphassajaṃ sañ‍ñaṃ, cakkhusamphassajaṃ caitanaṃ…pai॰… manausamphassajaṃ caitanaṃ, rūpataṇhaṃ  …pai॰… dhammataṇhaṃ, rūpavitak‍kaṃ…pai॰… dhammavitak‍kaṃ, rūpavicāraṃ…pai॰… dhammavicāra’’ntiādinā anupadadhammavibhāgavasainapi sabbaiva dhammakauṭṭhāsai anavasaisatau vami uggiri anapaikkhaparic‍cāgaina chaḍḍayi| vuttaṃ haitaṃ –

‘‘yaṃ taṃ, ānanda, cattaṃ vantaṃ muttaṃ pahīnaṃ paṭinissaṭṭhaṃ, taṃ tathāgatau puna pac‍cāvamissatīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jatī’’ti (dī॰ ni॰ 2.183)|

aivampi bhāgai vamīti bhagavā| atha vā bhāgai vamīti sabbaipi kusalākusalai sāvaj‍jānavaj‍jai hīnapaṇītai kaṇhasuk‍kasappaṭibhāgai dhammai ariyamaggañāṇamukhaina vami uggiri anapaikkhau paric‍caji pajahi, paraisañ‍ca tathattāya dhammaṃ daisaisi| vuttampi caitaṃ –

‘‘dhammāpi vau, bhikkhavai, pahātabbā, pagaiva adhammā (ma॰ ni॰ 1.240)| kul‍lūpamaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, dhammaṃ daisaissāmi nittharaṇatthāya, nau gahaṇatthāyā’’tiādi (ma॰ ni॰ 1.240)|

aivampi bhāgai vamīti bhagavā|

khandhāyatanadhātādi-dhammabhaidā mahaisinā|

kaṇhā suk‍kā yatau vantā, tataupi bhagavā matau||

taina vuttaṃ –

‘‘bhāgavā bhatavā bhāgai, bhagai ca vani bhattavā|

bhagai vami tathā bhāgai, vamīti bhagavā jinau’’ti||

aittha ca yasmā saṅkhaipatau attahitasampattiparahitapaṭipattivasaina duvidhā buddhaguṇā, tāsu attahitasampatti pahānasampadāñāṇasampadābhaidatau duvidhā, ānubhāvasampadādīnaṃ tadavinābhāvaina tadantaugadhattā| parahitapaṭipatti payaugāsayabhaidatau  duvidhā| tattha payaugatau lābhasak‍kārādinirapaikkhacittassa sabbadukkhaniyyānikadhammūpadaisau, āsayatau paṭiviruddhaisupi nic‍caṃ hitaisitā, ñāṇaparipākakālāgamanādiparahitapaṭipatti ca, āmisapaṭiggahaṇādināpi atthacariyā  parahitapaṭipatti hautiyaiva| tasmā taisaṃ vibhāvanavasaina pāḷiyaṃ ‘‘araha’’ntiādīnaṃ padānaṃ gahaṇaṃ vaiditabbaṃ|

tattha ‘‘araha’’nti iminā padaina pahānasampadāvasaina bhagavatau attahitasampatti vibhāvitā| ‘‘sammāsambuddhau, laukavidū’’ti ca imaihi padaihi ñāṇasampadāvasaina| nanu ca ‘‘laukavidū’’ti imināpi sammāsambuddhatā vibhāvīyatīti? sac‍caṃ vibhāvīyati, atthi pana visaisau, ‘‘sammāsambuddhau’’ti iminā sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇānubhāvau vibhāvitau, ‘‘laukavidū’’ti pana iminā āsayānusayañāṇādīnampi ānubhāvau vibhāvitauti| ‘‘vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau’’ti iminā sabbāpi bhagavatau attahitasampatti vibhāvitā| ‘‘sugatau’’ti pana iminā samudāgamatau paṭṭhāya bhagavatau attahitasampatti, parahitapaṭipatti ca vibhāvitā| ‘‘anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussāna’’nti imaihi padaihi bhagavatau parahitapaṭipatti vibhāvitā| ‘‘buddhau’’ti iminā bhagavatau attahitasampatti, parahitapaṭipatti ca vibhāvitā| aivañ‍ca katvā ‘‘sammāsambuddhau’’ti vatvā ‘‘buddhau’’ti vacanaṃ samatthitaṃ hauti| tainaivāha ‘‘attanāpi bujjhi añ‍ñaipi sattai baudhaisī’’tiādi| ‘‘bhagavā’’ti ca imināpi samudāgamatau paṭṭhāya bhagavatau sabbā attahitasampatti, parahitapaṭipatti ca vibhāvitā|

aparau nayau – haituphalasattūpakāravasaina saṅkhaipatau tividhā buddhaguṇā| tattha ‘‘arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau laukavidū’’ti imaihi padaihi phalasampattivasaina buddhaguṇā vibhāvitā| ‘‘anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussāna’’nti imaihi sattūpakāravasaina buddhaguṇā pakāsitā| ‘‘buddhau’’ti iminā phalavasaina, sattūpakāravasaina ca buddhaguṇā vibhāvitā| ‘‘sugatau bhagavā’’ti pana imaihi padaihi haituphalasattūpakāravasaina buddhaguṇā vibhāvitāti vaiditabbaṃ|

145. tassāti  buddhānussatikammaṭṭhānikassa yauginau| aivanti ‘‘tatrāyaṃ nayau’’tiādinā vuttappakāraina| tasmiṃ samayaiti buddhaguṇānussaraṇasamayai| rāgapariyuṭṭhitanti rāgaina pariyuṭṭhitaṃ, pariyuṭṭhānappattaihi rāgaihi sahitaṃ cittaṃ arañ‍ñamiva cauraihi taina pariyuṭṭhitanti vuttaṃ, tassa pariyuṭṭhānaṭṭhānabhāvatau, pariyuṭṭhitarāganti atthau| yaṃ vā rāgassa pariyuṭṭhānaṃ, taṃ taṃsahitai cittai upacaritanti daṭṭhabbaṃ| aitasmiṃ pakkhai rāgaināti rāgaina haitunāti  atthau| ujugatamaivāti pagaiva kāyavaṅkādīnaṃ apanītattā, cittassa ca anujubhāvakarānaṃ māyādīnaṃ abhāvatau, rāgādipariyuṭṭhānābhāvaina vā aunatiun‍nativirahatau ujubhāvamaiva kataṃ| atha vā ujugatamaivāti kammaṭṭhānassa vīthiṃ autiṇṇatāya līnuddhac‍cavigamanatau majjhimasamathanimittapaṭipattiyā ujubhāvamaiva gataṃ| tathāgataṃ ārabbhāti tathāgataguṇai ārammaṇaṃ katvā|

ic‍cassāti iti assa| ‘‘sau bhagavā itipi araha’’ntiādinā pubbai vuttappakāraina buddhaguṇai anuvitak‍kayatauti sambandhau| aivanti ‘‘naiva tasmiṃ samayai’’tiādinā vuttanayaina| buddhaguṇapauṇāti buddhaguṇanin‍nā| ‘‘vitak‍kavicārā pavattantī’’ti aitaina buddhaguṇasaṅkhātaṃ kammaṭṭhānaṃ vitak‍kāhataṃ, vitak‍kapariyāhataṃ, punappunaṃ anumaj‍janañ‍ca karautīti dassaiti| pīti uppaj‍jatīti bhāvanāvasaina upacārajjhānanipphādikā balavatī pīti uppaj‍jati| pītimanassa vuttappakārapītisahitacittassa| kāyacittadarathāti kāyacittapassaddhīnaṃ paṭipakkhabhūtā sukhumāvatthā uddhac‍casahagatā kilaisā| paṭippassambhantīti san‍nisīdanti| anuk‍kamaina aikakkhaṇaiti yadā buddhaguṇārammaṇā bhāvanā uparūpari visaisaṃ āvahantī pavattati, tadā nīvaraṇāni vikkhambhitānaiva haunti, kilaisā san‍nisin‍nāva haunti, suvisadāni saddhādīni indriyāni haunti, bhāvanāya paguṇatāya vitak‍kavicārā sātisayaṃ paṭukic‍cāva haunti, balavatī pīti uppaj‍jati, pītipadaṭṭhānā passaddhi savisaisā jāyati, passaddhakāyassa samādhipadaṭṭhānaṃ sukhaṃ thirataraṃ hutvā pavattati, sukhaina anubrūhitaṃ cittaṃ kammaṭṭhānai sammadaiva samādhiyati| aivaṃ anuk‍kamaina pubbabhāgai vitak‍kādayau uparūpari paṭupaṭutarabhāvaina pavattitvā bhāvanāya majjhimasamathapaṭipattiyā indriyānañ‍ca aikarasabhāvaina paṭutamāni hutvā aikakkhaṇai jhānaṅgāni uppaj‍janti| yaṃ sandhāya vuttaṃ bhagavatā –

‘‘yasmiṃ, mahānāma, samayai ariyasāvakau tathāgataṃ anussarati, naivassa tasmiṃ samayai rāgapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti…pai॰… sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyatī’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 6.10)|

kasmā panaittha jhānaṃ appanāpattaṃ na hautīti āha ‘‘buddhaguṇānaṃ pana gambhīratāyā’’tiādi| gambhīrai hi ārammaṇai gambhīrai udakai patitanāvā viya samathabhāvanā patiṭṭhaṃ  na labhati, ‘‘gambhīratāyā’’ti ca iminā sabhāvadhammatāpi saṅgahitā| sabhāvadhammau hi gambhīrau na pañ‍ñatti| nanu ca taj‍jāpañ‍ñattivasaina sabhāvadhammau gayhatīti? sac‍caṃ gayhati pubbabhāgai, bhāvanāya pana vaḍḍhamānāya pañ‍ñattiṃ samatik‍kamitvā sabhāvaiyaiva cittaṃ tiṭṭhati| nanu ca sabhāvadhammaipi katthaci appanā samijjhati dutiyāruppajjhānādīti? sac‍caṃ samijjhati, tañ‍ca khau aikappakārai ārammaṇai, idaṃ pana nānappakāranti dassaintau ‘‘nānappakāraguṇānussaraṇādhimuttatāyā’’ti āha| aivampi yathā nānappakāraisu kaisādikauṭṭhāsaisu manasikāraṃ pavattaintassa aikasmiṃyaiva kauṭṭhāsai appanāvasaina bhāvanā avatiṭṭhati, aivamidha kasmā na hautīti? visamauyaṃ upañ‍ñāsau| tattha hi satipi kauṭṭhāsabahubhāvai aikappakārainaiva bhāvanā vattati paṭik‍kūlākārassaiva gahaṇatau, idha pana nānappakāraina guṇānaṃ nānappakārattā, na ayamaikasmiṃyaiva guṇai ṭhātuṃ sak‍kauti bhāvanābalaina pac‍cakkhatau buddhaguṇaisu upaṭṭhahantaisu saddhāya balavabhāvatau| tainaiva hi ‘‘adhimuttatāyā’’ti vuttaṃ| yadi upacārappattaṃ jhānaṃ hauti, kathaṃ buddhānussatīti vuttanti āha ‘‘tadaita’’ntiādi|

sagāravau hauti sappatissau, satthu guṇasarīrassa pac‍cakkhatau upaṭṭhahanatau| tatauyaiva saddhāvaipul‍laṃ avaic‍cappasādasadisaṃ saddhāmahattaṃ| buddhaguṇānaṃ anussaraṇavasaina bhāvanāya pavattattā sativaipul‍laṃ| taisaṃ paramagambhīratāya, nānappakāratāya ca pañ‍ñāvaipul‍laṃ| taisaṃ uḷārapuñ‍ñakkhaittatāya puñ‍ñavaipul‍lañ‍ca adhigacchati| pītipāmauj‍jabahulau hauti pītisambaujjhaṅgaṭṭhānīyattā buddhānussatiyā| bhayabhairavasahau buddhaguṇārammaṇāya satiyā bhayabhairavābhibhavanatau| taināha bhagavā ‘‘anussaraitha sambuddhaṃ, bhayaṃ tumhāka nau siyā’’ti (saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.249 thaukaṃ visadisaṃ)| dukkhādhivāsanasamatthau buddhānussatiyā sarīradukkhassa padumapalāsai udakassa viya vinivaṭṭanatau| saṃvāsasañ‍ñanti sahavāsasañ‍ñaṃ| buddhabhūmiyaṃ cittaṃ namati buddhaguṇānaṃ mahantabhāvassa pac‍cakkhatau upaṭṭhānatau| buddhānussatibhāvanāya sabbadā buddhaguṇānaṃ cittai viparivattanatau vuttaṃ ‘‘sammukhā satthāraṃ passatau viyā’’ti| uttari appaṭivijjhantauti imāya bhāvanāya upari taṃ adhiṭṭhānaṃ katvā sac‍cāni appaṭivijjhantau| sugatiparāyaṇauti sugatisamparāyau|

appamādanti appamaj‍janaṃ sak‍kac‍caṃ anuyaugaṃ| kayirāthāti karaiyya| sumaidhasauti sundarapañ‍ñau| aivaṃ mahānubhāvāyāti vuttappakāraina nīvaraṇavikkhambhanādisamatthaina satthari sagāravabhāvādihaitubhūtaina mahatā sāmatthiyaina saman‍nāgatāya|

2. dhammānussatikathāvaṇṇanā

146. dhammānussatinti aittha padatthau, sabhāvatthau ca āditau vuttauyaivāti taṃ anāmasitvā bhāvanāvidhānamaiva dassaituṃ ‘‘dhammānussatiṃ bhāvaitukāmaināpī’’tiādi āraddhaṃ| kasmā panaittha buddhānussatiyaṃ viya ‘‘avaic‍cappasādasaman‍nāgatainā’’ti na vuttaṃ| nanu aitā paṭipāṭiyā cha anussatiyau ariyasāvakānaṃ vasaina daisanaṃ āruḷhāti? sac‍camaitaṃ, aivaṃ santaipi ‘‘parisuddhasīlādiguṇānaṃ puthuj‍janānampi ijjhantī’’ti dassanatthaṃ ‘‘avaic‍cappasādasaman‍nāgatainā’’ti idha na vuttaṃ, ariyasāvakānaṃ pana sukhaina ijjhantīti| tathāpi pāḷiyaṃ āgatattā ca buddhānussatiniddaisai vuttanti daṭṭhabbaṃ, ayañ‍ca vicārau paratau āgamissataiva|

147. pariyattidhammaupīti pi-saddaina laukuttaradhammaṃ sampiṇḍaiti| itaraisūti sandiṭṭhikādipadaisu| laukuttaradhammauvāti avadhāraṇaṃ nippariyāyaina sandiṭṭhikādiatthaṃ sandhāya vuttaṃ, pariyāyatau panaitai pariyattidhammaipi sambhavantaiva| tathā hi pariyattidhammau bahussutaina āgatāgamaina paramaina satinaipak‍kaina saman‍nāgataina pañ‍ñavatā ādikalyāṇatādivisaisatau sayaṃ daṭṭhabbauti sandiṭṭhikau| ācariyapayirupāsanāya vinā na labbhāti cai? laukuttaradhammaipi samānai kalyāṇamittasan‍nissayainaiva sijjhanatau, tathā satthusandassanaipi| ‘‘sandiṭṭhiyā jayatī’’ti ayaṃ panatthau titthiyanimmaddanai nippariyāyatauva labbhati, kilaisajayai pariyāyatau paramparāhaitubhāvatau| ‘‘sandiṭṭhaṃ arahatī’’ti ayampi atthau labbhataiva, sabbasau saṃkilaisadhammānaṃ pahānaṃ, vaudānadhammānaṃ paribrūhanañ‍ca uddissa pavattattā| ayañ‍ca atthau ‘‘yai khau tvaṃ, gautami, dhammai jānaiyyāsi, imai dhammā sarāgāya saṃvattanti nau virāgāya, sañ‍ñaugāya saṃvattanti nau visañ‍ñaugāya, saupādānāya saṃvattanti nau anupādānāya| aikaṃsaina, gautami, dhāraiyyāsi ‘naisau dhammau, naisau vinayau, naitaṃ satthusāsana’’’nti (cūḷava॰ 406; a॰ ni॰ 8.53), ‘‘dhammāpi vau, bhikkhavai, pahātabbā, pagaiva adhammā’’ti (ma॰ ni॰ 1.240) ca aivamādīhi suttapadaihi vibhāvaitabbau| akālikādhigamupāyatāya akālikau| vij‍jamānatāparisuddhatāhi aihipassavidhiṃ arahatīti aihipassikau| tatau aiva vaṭṭadukkhanittharaṇatthikaihi  attanau cittai upanayanaṃ, cittassa vā tattha upanayanaṃ arahatīti aupanaiyyikau| vimuttāyatanasīsai ṭhatvā paricayaina sammadaiva atthavaidaṃ, dhammavaidañ‍ca labhantaihi pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbau viñ‍ñūhīti| aivaṃ sandiṭṭhikādipadaisu pariyattidhammampi pakkhipitvā manasikārau yuj‍jataiva|

sāti gāthā| samantabhadrakattāti sabbabhāgaihi sundarattā| dhammassāti sāsanadhammassa| kiñ‍cāpi avayavavinimuttau samudāyau nāma paramatthatau kauci natthi, yaisu pana avayavaisu samudāyarūpaina avaikkhitaisu ‘‘gāthā’’ti samañ‍ñā, taṃ tatau bhin‍naṃ viya katvā saṃsāmivauhāraṃ āraupaitvā dassaintau ‘‘paṭhamaina pādaina ādikalyāṇā’’tiādimāha| ‘‘aikānusandhika’’nti idaṃ nātibahuvibhāgaṃ yathānusandhinā aikānusandhikaṃ sandhāya vuttaṃ, itarassa pana tainaiva daisaitabbadhammavibhāgaina ādimajjhapariyausānabhāgā labbhantīti| nidānaināti kāladaisadaisakaparisādiapadisanalakkhaṇaina nidānaganthaina| nigamanaināti ‘‘idamavaucā’’tiādikaina (saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.249), ‘‘iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ, idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vutta’’nti (a॰ ni॰ 10.27) vā yathāvuttatthanigamanaina|

ayaṃ kathā visaisatau suttapiṭakasaṃvaṇṇanāti katvā suttapiṭakavasaina dhammassa ādikalyāṇādikaṃ dassaitvā idāni suttapiṭakavinayapiṭakānaṃ vasaina taṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘sanidānasauppattikattā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tattha sanidānasauppattikattāti yathāvuttanidānaina sanidānatāya, saaṭṭhuppattikatāya  ca| vainaiyyānaṃ anurūpatauti sikkhāpadapañ‍ñattiyā, dhammadaisanāya ca taṃtaṃsikkhāpadabhāvaina pañ‍ñāpiyamānassa vainaiyyajjhāsayānurūpaṃ pavattiyamānassa anurūpatau| atthassāti daisiyamānassa ca sīlādiatthassa| ‘‘taṃ kissa haitu (saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.249)? saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai’’tiādinā (saṃ॰ ni॰ 3.95) tattha tattha haitūpamāgahaṇaina haitudāharaṇayuttatau|

aivaṃ suttavinayavasaina pariyattidhammassa ādimajjhapariyausānakalyāṇataṃ dassaitvā idāni tīṇi piṭakāni aikajjhaṃ gahaitvā taṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘sakalaupī’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tattha sāsanadhammauti –

‘‘sabbapāpassa  akaraṇaṃ, kusalassa upasampadā|

sacittapariyaudapanaṃ, aitaṃ buddhāna sāsana’’nti|| (dha॰ pa॰ 183; dī॰ ni॰ 2.90) –

aivaṃ vuttassa satthusāsanassa pakāsakau pariyattidhammau| sīlaina ādikalyāṇau sīlamūlakattā sāsanassa| samathādīhi majjhaikalyāṇau taisaṃ sāsanasampattiyā vaimajjhabhāvatau| nibbānaina pariyausānakalyāṇau tadadhigamatau uttarikaraṇīyābhāvatau| sāsanai sammāpaṭipatti nāma pañ‍ñāya hauti, tassā ca sīlaṃ, samādhi ca mūlanti āha ‘‘sīlasamādhīhi ādikalyāṇau’’ti| pañ‍ñā pana anubaudhapaṭivaidhavasaina duvidhāti tadubhayampi gaṇhantau ‘‘vipassanāmaggaihi majjhaikalyāṇau’’ti āha| tassa nipphatti phalaṃ, kic‍caṃ nibbānasacchikiriyā, tatau paraṃ kattabbaṃ natthīti dassaintau āha ‘‘phalanibbānaihi pariyausānakalyāṇau’’ti| phalaggahaṇaina vā saupādisaisanibbānamāha, itaraina itaraṃ, tadubhayañ‍ca sāsanasampattiyā ausānanti āha ‘‘phalanibbānaihi pariyausānakalyāṇau’’ti|

buddhassa subaudhitā sammāsambuddhatā, tāya ādikalyāṇau tappabhavattā| sabbasau saṃkilaisappahānaṃ vaudānaṃ, pāripūrī ca dhammasudhammatā, tāya majjhaikalyāṇau taṃsarīrattā| satthārā yathānusiṭṭhaṃ tathā paṭipatti saṅghasuppaṭipatti, tāya pariyausānakalyāṇau, tāya sāsanassa laukai suppatiṭṭhitabhāvatau| tanti sāsanadhammaṃ| tathattāyāti yathattāya bhagavatā dhammau daisitau, tathattāya tathabhāvāya| sau pana abhisambaudhi pac‍caikabaudhi sāvakabaudhīti tividhau, itau añ‍ñathā nibbānādhigamassa abhāvatau| tattha sabbaguṇaihi aggabhāvatau, itarabaudhidvayamūlatāya ca paṭhamāya baudhiyā ādikalyāṇatā, guṇaihi vaimajjhabhāvatau dutiyāya majjhaikalyāṇatā, tadubhayāvaratāya, tadausānatāya ca sāsanadhammassa tatiyāya pariyausānakalyāṇatā vuttā|

aisauti sāsanadhammau| nīvaraṇavikkhambhanatauti vimuttāyatanasīsai ṭhatvā saddhammaṃ suṇantassa nīvaraṇānaṃ vikkhambhanasambhavatau| vuttaṃ haitaṃ –

‘‘yathā yathāvusau, bhikkhunau satthā vā dhammaṃ daisaiti, añ‍ñatarau vā garuṭṭhāniyau sabrahmacārī, tathā tathā sau tattha labhati atthavaidaṃ labhati dhammavaida’’nti (a॰ ni॰ 5.26)|

‘‘yasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, samayai ariyasāvakau auhitasautau dhammaṃ suṇāti, pañ‍cassa nīvaraṇāni tasmiṃ samayai pahīnāni hauntī’’ti (saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.219) –

ca ādi| samathavipassanāsukhāvahanatauti samathasukhassa ca vipassanāsukhassa ca sampādanatau| vuttampi caitaṃ –

‘‘sau vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukha’’ntiādi (dī॰ ni॰ 1.279; ma॰ ni॰ 2.138)|

tathā –

‘‘yatau yatau sammasati, khandhānaṃ udayabbayaṃ|

labhatī pītipāmauj‍jaṃ, amataṃ taṃ vijānataṃ|

amānusī ratī hauti, sammā dhammaṃ vipassatau’’ti ca|| (dha॰ pa॰ 374, 373)|

tathāpaṭipan‍nauti yathā samathavipassanāsukhaṃ āvahati, yathā vā satthārā anusiṭṭhaṃ, tathā paṭipan‍nau sāsanadhammau| tādibhāvāvahanatauti chaḷaṅgupaikkhāvasaina iṭṭhādīsu tādibhāvassa laukadhammaihi anupalaipassa āvahanatau| aisa bhagavā vuttanayaina tividhakalyāṇaṃ dhammaṃ daisaintau yaṃ sāsanabrahmacariyaṃ, maggabrahmacariyañ‍ca pakāsaiti, taṃ yathānurūpaṃ atthasampattiyā sātthaṃ, byañ‍janasampattiyā sabyañ‍jananti yaujanā|

tattha  avisaisaina tissau sikkhā, sakalau ca tantidhammau sāsanabrahmacariyaṃ| yaṃ sandhāya vuttaṃ ‘‘katamaisānaṃ khau, bhantai, buddhānaṃ bhagavantānaṃ brahmacariyaṃ naciraṭṭhitikaṃ ahausī’’tiādi (pārā॰ 18)| sabbasikkhānaṃ maṇḍabhūtasikkhattayasaṅgahitau ariyamaggau maggabrahmacariyaṃ| yaṃ sandhāya  vuttaṃ ‘‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīya’’nti (mahāva॰ 23; saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.186)| yathānurūpanti yathārahaṃ| sikkhattayasaṅgahaṃ hi sāsanabrahmacariyaṃ atthasampattiyā sātthaṃ| maggabrahmacariyai vattabbamaiva natthi| atthasampattiyāti sampan‍natthatāya| sampattiatthau hi idha saha-saddau| itaraṃ pana yathāvuttainatthaina sātthaṃ, sabyañ‍janañ‍ca| yai panaittha ‘‘vacanasabhāvaṃ sātthaṃ, atthasabhāvaṃ sabyañ‍jana’’nti vibhajitvā vadanti, taṃ na sundaraṃ, tathā vibhattassa pariyattidhammassa abhāvatau| saddatthā hi abhin‍narūpā viya hutvā viniyaugaṃ gacchanti| tathā hi naisaṃ laukiyāmissībhāvaṃ paṭijānanti| satipi vā bhaidai ‘‘sabyañ‍jana’’nti aittha yadi tulyayaugau adhippaitau ‘‘saputtau āgatau’’tiādīsu viya, aivaṃ sati ‘‘atthapaṭisaraṇā, bhikkhavai, hautha, mā byañ‍janapaṭisaraṇā’’ti atthappadhānavādau bādhitau siyā, atha vij‍jamānatāmattaṃ ‘‘salaumakau sapakkhakau’’tiādīsu viya byañ‍janasampatti aggahitā siyā| tasmā aṭṭhakathāyaṃ vuttanayainaiva atthau gahaitabbau|

sātthaṃ sabyañ‍jananti aittha naittinayaināpi atthaṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘saṅkāsana…pai॰… sabyañ‍jana’’nti vuttaṃ| tattha yadipi naittiyaṃ byañ‍janamukhaina byañ‍janatthaggahaṇaṃ hautīti ‘‘akkharaṃ pada’’ntiādinā (naitti॰ 4 dvādasapada) byañ‍janapadāni paṭhamaṃ uddiṭṭhāni, idha pana pāḷiyaṃ ‘‘sātthaṃ sabyañ‍jana’’nti āgatattā atthapadāniyaiva paṭhamaṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘saṅkāsanapakāsanā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tattha saṅkhaipatau kāsanaṃ dīpanaṃ saṅkāsanaṃ ‘‘mañ‍ñamānau bhikkhu baddhau mārassa, amañ‍ñamānau muttau’’tiādīsu (saṃ॰ ni॰ 3.64) viya| tattakaina hi taina bhikkhunā paṭividdhaṃ| taināha ‘‘añ‍ñātaṃ bhagavā’’tiādi| paṭhamaṃ kāsanaṃ pakāsanaṃ| ādikammasmiṃ hi ayaṃ pasaddau ‘‘pañ‍ñapaiti paṭṭhapaitī’’tiādīsu (saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.20) viya| tikkhindriyāpaikkhaṃ caitaṃ padadvayaṃ uddaisabhāvatau| tikkhindriyau hi saṅkhaipatau paṭhamañ‍ca vuttamatthaṃ paṭipaj‍jati| saṃkhittassa vitthāravacanaṃ, sakiṃ vuttassa puna vacanañ‍ca vivaraṇavibhajanāni| yathā ‘‘kusalā dhammā’’ti saṅkhaipatau sakiṃyaiva ca vuttassa atthassa ‘‘katamai dhammā kusalā? yasmiṃ samayai kāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ citta’’ntiādinā (dha॰ sa॰ 1) vitthāratau vivaraṇavasaina, vibhajanavasaina ca puna vacanaṃ| majjhimindriyāpaikkhamaitaṃ padadvayaṃ niddaisabhāvatau| vivaṭassa vitthāratarābhidhānaṃ, vibhattassa ca pakāraihi ñāpanaṃ vainaiyyānaṃ cittaparitausanaṃ uttānīkaraṇapañ‍ñāpanāni| yathā ‘‘phassau hautī’’tiādinā (dha॰ sa॰ 2) vivaṭavibhattassa atthassa ‘‘katamau tasmiṃ samayai phassau? yau tasmiṃ samayai phassau phusanā samphusanā’’tiādinā  (dha॰ sa॰ 2) uttānīkiriyā, pañ‍ñāpanā ca| mudindriyāpaikkhamaitaṃ padadvayaṃ paṭiniddaisabhāvatau| ‘‘pañ‍ñāpanapaṭṭhapanavivaraṇavibhajanauttānīkaraṇapakāsanaatthapadasamāyaugatau’’tipi pāṭhau|

kathaṃ panāyaṃ pāṭhavikappau jātauti? vuc‍catai – naittipāḷiyaṃ āgatanayaina purimapāṭhau| tattha hi –

‘‘saṅkāsanā pakāsanā,

vivaraṇā vibhajanuttānīkammapañ‍ñatti|

aitaihi chahi padaihi,

atthau kammañ‍ca niddiṭṭha’’nti|| (naitti॰ 4 dvādasapada) –

daisanāhārayaujanāya ca aitassaivatthassa ‘‘saṅkāsanā pakāsanā’’tiādinā āgataṃ| pacchimau pana ‘‘pañ‍ñapaiti paṭṭhapaiti vivarati vibhajati uttānīkarauti ‘passathā’ti cāhā’’ti (saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.20) suttai āgatanayaina byañ‍janamattakatau| aivañ‍ca naisaṃ dvin‍naṃ pāṭhānaṃ visaisau, na atthatau| tathā hi saṅkhaipatau ‘‘paṭhamaṃ ñāpanaṃ pañ‍ñāpanaṃ, paṭhamamaiva ṭhapanaṃ paṭṭhapana’’nti imāni padāni saṅkāsanapakāsanapadaihi atthatau avisiṭṭhāni| yañ‍ca purimapāṭhai chaṭṭhaṃ padaṃ ‘‘pakāratau ñāpana’’nti pañ‍ñāpanaṃ vuttaṃ, taṃ dutiyapāṭhai pakāsanapadaina ‘‘nibbisaisaṃ pakāratau kāsana’’nti katvā| yasmā ñāpanakāsanāni atthāvabhāsanasabhāvatāya abhin‍nāni, sabbaisañ‍ca vainaiyyānaṃ cittassa tausanaṃ, buddhiyā ca nisānaṃ yāthāvatau vatthusabhāvāvabhāsanai jāyatīti| aivaṃ atthapadarūpattā pariyattiatthassa yathāvuttachaatthapadasamāyaugatau sātthaṃ sāsanaṃ|

akkharapadabyañ‍janākāraniruttiniddaisasampattiyāti  aittha uc‍cāraṇavailāyaṃ apariyausitai padai vaṇṇau akkharaṃ| ‘‘aikakkharaṃ padaṃ akkhara’’nti aikai ‘‘ā aivaṃ kira ta’’ntiādīsu ā-kārādayau viya| ‘‘visuddhakaraṇānaṃ manasā daisanāvācāya akkharaṇatau akkhara’’nti añ‍ñai| vibhattiyantaṃ atthañāpanatau padaṃ| saṅkhaipatau vuttaṃ padābhihitaṃ atthaṃ byañ‍jaitīti byañ‍janaṃ, vākyaṃ| ‘‘kiriyāpadaṃ abyayakārakavisaisanayuttaṃ vākya’’nti hi vadanti| pakāratau vākyavibhāgau  ākārau| ākārābhihitaṃ nibbacanaṃ nirutti| nibbacanavitthārau nissaisupadaisatau niddaisau| aitaisaṃ akkharādīnaṃ byañ‍janapadānaṃ sampattiyā sampan‍natāya sabyañ‍janaṃ|

tatrāyamassa atthapadasamāyaugau, byañ‍janasampatti ca – akkharaihi saṅkāsaiti, padaihi pakāsaiti, byañ‍janaihi vivarati, ākāraihi vibhajati, niruttīhi uttānīkarauti, niddaisaihi pañ‍ñapaiti, tathā akkharaihi ugghāṭaitvā padaihi vinaiti ugghaṭitañ‍ñuṃ, byañ‍janaihi vipañ‍caitvā ākāraihi vinaiti vipañ‍citañ‍ñuṃ, niruttīhi naitvā niddaisaihi vinaiti naiyyaṃ| aivañ‍cāyaṃ dhammau ugghaṭiyamānau ugghaṭitañ‍ñuṃ vinaiti, vipañ‍ciyamānau vipañ‍citañ‍ñuṃ, niyyamānau naiyyaṃ| tattha ugghaṭanā ādi, vipañ‍canā majjhai, nayanamantai| aivaṃ tīsu kālaisu tidhā daisitau dausattayavidhamanau guṇattayāvahau tividhavainaiyyavinayanauti| aivampi tividhakalyāṇauyaṃ dhammau atthabyañ‍janapāripūriyā ‘‘sātthau sabyañ‍janau’’ti vaiditabbau ‘‘paripuṇṇau, parisuddhau’’ti ca|

atthagambhīratātiādīsu atthau nāma tantiatthau| dhammau tanti| paṭivaidhau tantiyā, tantiatthassa ca yathābhūtāvabaudhau| daisanā manasā vavatthāpitāya tantiyā daisanā| tai panaitai atthādayau yasmā sasādīhi viya mahāsamuddau mandabuddhīhi dukkhaugāhā, alabbhanaiyyapatiṭṭhā ca, tasmā gambhīrā| atha vā atthau nāma haituphalaṃ| dhammau haitu| daisanā pañ‍ñatti, yathādhammaṃ dhammābhilāpau, anulaumapaṭilaumasaṅkhaipavitthārādivasaina vā kathanaṃ| paṭivaidhau abhisamayau, atthānurūpaṃ dhammaisu, dhammānurūpaṃ atthaisu, pañ‍ñattipathānurūpaṃ pañ‍ñattīsu avabaudhau, taisaṃ taisaṃ vā dhammānaṃ paṭivijjhitabbau salakkhaṇasaṅkhātau aviparītasabhāvau| taipi caitai atthādayau yasmā anupacitakusalasambhāraihi duppañ‍ñaihi  sasādīhi viya mahāsamuddau dukkhaugāhā, alabbhanaiyyapatiṭṭhā ca, tasmā gambhīrā| taisu paṭivaidhassāpi atthasan‍nissitattā vuttaṃ ‘‘atthagambhīratāpaṭivaidhagambhīratāhi sāttha’’nti atthaguṇadīpanatau| tāsaṃ dhammadaisanānaṃ byañ‍janasan‍nissitattā vuttaṃ ‘‘dhammagambhīratādaisanāgambhīratāhi sabyañ‍jana’’nti tāsaṃ byañ‍janasampattidīpanatau|

atthaisu pabhaidagataṃ ñāṇaṃ atthapaṭisambhidā| atthadhammaniruttipaṭisambhidāsu pabhaidagataṃ ñāṇaṃ paṭibhānapaṭisambhidāti imissāpi paṭisambhidāya atthavisayattā āha ‘‘atthapaṭibhānapaṭisambhidāvisayatau sāttha’’nti, atthasampattiyā asati tadabhāvatau| dhammauti tanti| niruttīti tantipadānaṃ niddhāraitvā vacanaṃ| tattha pabhaidagatāni ñāṇāni dhammaniruttipaṭisambhidāti āha ‘‘dhammaniruttipaṭisambhidāvisayatau sabyañ‍jananti, asati byañ‍janasampattiyā tadabhāvatau| parikkhakajanappasādakanti aittha iti-saddau haituatthau| yasmā parikkhakajanānaṃ kiṃkusalagavaisīnaṃ pasādāvahaṃ, tasmā sātthaṃ| atthasampan‍nanti phalaina haitunau anumānaṃ nadīpūraina viya upari vuṭṭhippavattiyā| sātthakatā panassa paṇḍitavaidanīyatāya, sā paramagambhīrasaṇhasukhumabhāvatau vaiditabbā| vuttañhaitaṃ ‘‘gambhīrau duddasau’’tiādi (mahāva॰ 8)| laukiyajanappasādakanti sabyañ‍jananti yasmā laukiyajanassa pasādāvahaṃ, tasmā sabyañ‍janaṃ| laukiyajanau hi byañ‍janasampattiyā tussati, idhāpi phalaina haitunau anumānaṃ| sabyañ‍janatā panassa saddhaiyyatāya, sā ādikalyāṇādibhāvatau vaiditabbā|

atha vā paṇḍitavaidanīyatau sāttha’’nti pañ‍ñāpadaṭṭhānatāya atthasampan‍nataṃ āha, tatau parikkhakajanappasādakaṃ| saddhaiyyatau sabyañ‍jananti saddhāpadaṭṭhānatāya byañ‍janasampan‍nataṃ, tatau laukiyajanappasādakanti aivamaittha atthau daṭṭhabbau| gambhīrādhippāyatau sātthanti adhippāyatau agādhāpāratāya atthasampan‍naṃ añ‍ñathā tadabhāvatau| uttānapadatau sabyañ‍jananti subaudhasaddatāya byañ‍janasampan‍naṃ, paramagambhīrassapi atthassa vainaiyyānaṃ suviñ‍ñaiyyabhāvāpādanatau| sabbaupaisa paṭhamassa atthadvayassa pabhaidau daṭṭhabbau, tathā caiva tattha tattha saṃvaṇṇitaṃ| tathā haittha  vikappassa, samuc‍cayassa vā aggahaṇaṃ| upanaitabbassāti pakkhipitabbassa vaudānatthassa avuttassa abhāvatau| sakalaparipuṇṇabhāvaināti sabbabhāgaihi paripuṇṇatāya| apanaitabbassāti saṃkilaisadhammassa|

paṭipattiyāti sīlavisuddhiyādisammāpaṭipattiyā, tan‍nimittaṃ| adhigamabyattitauti sac‍capaṭivaidhaina adhigamavaiyyattiyasabbhāvatau sātthaṃ kapilavatādi viya tucchaṃ niratthakaṃ ahutvā atthasampan‍nanti katvā| pariyattiyāti pariyattidhammaparicayaina| āgamabyattitauti durakkhātadhammaisu paricayaṃ karauntassa viya sammauhaṃ ajanaitvā bāhusac‍cavaiyyattiyasabbhāvatau sabyañ‍canaṃ| byañ‍janasampattiyā hi sati āgamabyattīti| sīlādipañ‍cadhammakkhandhayuttatauti sīlādīhi pañ‍cahi dhammakauṭṭhāsaihi avirahitattā| kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ anavasaisaina samantatau puṇṇaṃ pūritaṃ| nirupak‍kilaisatauti diṭṭhimānādiupak‍kilaisābhāvatau| nittharaṇatthāyāti vaṭṭadukkhatau nissaraṇāya| laukāmisanirapaikkhatauti kathañ‍cipi taṇhāsan‍nissayassa anissayanatau|

aivaṃ  ādikalyāṇatādiapadaisaina satthu purimavaisāraj‍jadvayavasaina dhammassa svākkhātataṃ vibhāvaitvā idāni pacchimavaisāraj‍jadvayavasaināpi taṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘atthavipal‍lāsābhāvatau vā suṭṭhu akkhātauti svākkhātau’’ti vatvā tamatthaṃ byatiraikamukhaina vibhāvaintau ‘‘yathā hī’’tiādimāha| tattha vipal‍lāsamāpaj‍jatīti taisaṃ dhammai ‘‘antarāyikā’’ti vuttadhammānaṃ vipākādīnaṃ antarāyikattābhāvatau aikaṃsaina apāyūpapattihaitutāya abhāvatau| niyyānikattābhāvatauti atammayatābhāvatau, saṃsāratau ca niyyānikāti vuttadhammānaṃ aikac‍cayañ‍ñakiriyāpakatipurisantarañ‍ñāṇādīnaṃ tatau niyyānikattābhāvatau viparītau aiva hauti| taināti atthassa vipal‍lāsāpaj‍janaina| tai añ‍ñatitthiyā| tathābhāvānatik‍kamanatauti kammantarāyādīnaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ antarāyikabhāvassa ariyamaggadhammānaṃ niyyānikabhāvassa kadācipi anativattanatau|

nibbānānurūpāya paṭipattiyāti adhigantabbassa sabbasaṅkhatavinissaṭassa nibbānassa anurūpāya sabbasaṅkhāranissaraṇūpāyabhūtāya sapubbabhāgāya sammāpaṭipattiyā  akkhātattāti yaujanā| paṭipadānurūpassāti sabbadukkhaniyyānikabhūtā ārammaṇakaraṇamattaināpi kilaisaihi anāmasanīyā yādisī paṭipadā, tadanurūpassa| supañ‍ñattāti sīlādikkhandhattayasaṅgahitā sammādiṭṭhiādippabhaidā micchādiṭṭhiādīnaṃ pahāyikabhāvaina suṭṭhu sammadaiva vihitā| saṃsandati kilaisamalavisuddhitāya samaiti| aivaṃ magganibbānānaṃ paṭipadāpaṭipaj‍janīyabhāvaihi añ‍ñamañ‍ñānurūpatāya svākkhātataṃ dassaitvā idāni tividhassāpi laukuttaradhammassa pac‍caikaṃ svākkhātataṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘ariyamaggau caitthā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tattha antadvayanti sassatucchaidaṃ, kāmasukhaattakilamathānuyaugaṃ, līnuddhac‍caṃ, patiṭṭhānāyūhananti aivaṃ pabhaidaṃ antadvayaṃ| anupagammāti anupagantvā, anupagamanahaitu vā| paṭipassaddhakilaisānīti suṭṭhu vūpasantakilaisāni, paṭipassaddhippahānavasaina sammadaiva pahīnadausāni| sassatādisabhāvavasaināti ‘‘sassatañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, dhammaṃ daisaissāmi amatañ‍ca tāṇañ‍ca laiṇañ‍cā’’tiādinā taisu taisu suttaisu sassatādibhāvakittanavasaina|

148. ‘‘rāgādīnaṃ abhāvaṃ karauntaina ariyapuggalaina sāmaṃ daṭṭhabbau’’ti iminā ‘‘ariyamaggaina mama rāgādayau pahīnā’’ti sayaṃ attanā anañ‍ñanaiyyaina daṭṭhabbauti sandiṭṭhi, sandiṭṭhi aiva sandiṭṭhikau|

taina  taina ariyasāvakaina parasaddhāya parassa saddahanaina paranaiyyaina gantabbataṃ hitvā ñāpaitabbataṃ pahāya pac‍cavaikkhaṇañāṇaina karaṇabhūtaina| pasatthā diṭṭhi sandiṭṭhi yathā sambaujjhaṅgau| ariyamaggau attanā sampayuttāya sandiṭṭhiyā kilaisai jayati, ariyaphalaṃ tāya aiva attanau kāraṇabhūtāya, nibbānaṃ ārammaṇakaraṇaina attanau visayībhūtāya sandiṭṭhiyā kilaisai jayatīti yaujanā|

bhāvanābhisamayavasaina maggadhammau| sacchikiriyābhisamayavasaina nibbānadhammau| phalampi haiṭṭhimaṃ sakadāgāmivipassanādīnaṃ pac‍cayabhāvaina upari maggādhigamassa upanissayabhāvatau pariyāyatau ‘‘dissamānau vaṭṭabhayaṃ nivattaitī’’ti vattabbataṃ labhati|

149. nāssa  kālauti nāssa āgamaitabbau kālau atthi| yathā hi laukiyakusalassa ‘‘upapaj‍jai, aparapariyāyai’’tiādinā phaladānaṃ pati āgamaitabbau kālau atthi, na aivamaitassāti atthau| taināha ‘‘na pañ‍cāhā’’tiādi| pakaṭṭhauti dūrau| phaladānaṃ pati kālau pakaṭṭhau assāti kālikau, kālantaraphaladāyī| taināha ‘‘attanau phaladānai’’ti| pattauti upanītau| idanti ‘‘akālikau’’ti padaṃ|

150. vidhinti vidhānaṃ, ‘‘aihi passā’’ti aivaṃpavattavidhivacanaṃ| vij‍jamānattāti paramatthatau upalabbhamānattā| parisuddhattāti kilaisamalavirahaina sabbathā visuddhattā| amanuñ‍ñampi kadāci payaujanavasaina yathāsabhāvappakāsanaina dassaitabbaṃ bhavaiyyāti tadabhāvaṃ dassaintau āha ‘‘manuñ‍ñabhāvappakāsanainā’’ti|

151. upanaitabbauti upanaiyyau, upanaiyyauva aupanaiyyikauti imassa atthassa adhippaitattā āha ‘‘upanaitabbauti aupanaiyyikau’’ti| cittai upanayanaṃ uppādananti āha ‘‘idaṃ saṅkhatai laukuttaradhammai yuj‍jatī’’ti| cittai upanayananti pana ārammaṇabhūtassa dhammassa ārammaṇabhāvūpanayanai adhippaitai asaṅkhataipi yuj‍jaiyya ‘‘ārammaṇakaraṇasaṅkhātaṃ upanayanaṃ arahatīti aupanaiyyikau’’ti| al‍līyananti phusanaṃ| nibbānaṃ upanaiti ariyapuggalanti adhippāyau|

152. viñ‍ñūhīti  vidūhi, paṭividdhasac‍caihīti atthau| tai pana aikaṃsatau ugghaṭitañ‍ñūādayau hauntīti āha ‘‘ugghaṭitañ‍ñūādīhī’’ti| ‘‘pac‍catta’’nti aitassa ‘‘pati attanī’’ti bhummavasaina atthau gahaitabbauti āha ‘‘attani attanī’’ti| vaiditabbauti vuttaṃ, kathaṃ vaiditabbauti āha ‘‘bhāvitau mai’’tiādi| tattha ‘‘mai’’ti iminā ‘‘pac‍catta’’nti padaina vuttamatthaṃ anvayatau dassaitvā puna byatiraikaina dassaituṃ ‘‘na hī’’tiādi vuttaṃ, taṃ suviñ‍ñaiyyamaiva| ‘‘viñ‍ñūhī’’ti idaṃ antaugadhāvadhāraṇanti dassaintau ‘‘bālānaṃ pana avisayau caisā’’ti āha|

aivaṃ ādikalyāṇatādinā añ‍ñamañ‍ñassa anurūpadaisanāya dhammassa svākkhātataṃ dassaitvā idāni purimassa purimassa pacchimaṃ pacchimaṃ padaṃ kāraṇavacananti dassaintau ‘‘apicā’’tiādimāha| tattha sandiṭṭhikattāti yasmā  ayaṃ dhammau vuttanayaina sāmaṃ daṭṭhabbau, sandiṭṭhiyā kilaisai viddhaṃsaiti, sandassanañ‍ca arahati, tasmā svākkhātau durakkhātai titthiyadhammai tadabhāvatau| iminā nayaina saisapadaisupi yathārahaṃ atthau daṭṭhabbau|

153. tassaivantiādīsu yaṃ vattabbaṃ, taṃ buddhānussatiyaṃ vuttanayaina vaiditabbaṃ|

3. saṅghānussatikathāvaṇṇanā

154. ariyasaṅghaguṇāti ārakattā kilaisaihi, anayai na iriyanatau, ayai ca iriyanatau, sadaivakaina ca laukaina ‘‘saraṇa’’nti araṇīyatau ariyau ca sau saṅghau ca, ariyānaṃ vā saṅghau ariyasaṅghau, tassa guṇā|

155. yaṃ sammāpaṭipadaṃ paṭipan‍nau ‘‘suṭṭhu paṭipan‍nau’’ti vuc‍cati| sā ariyamaggapaṭipadā paṭipakkhadhammai anivattidhammai katvā pajahanatau payaujanābhāvā sayampi anivattidhammā, adhigantabbassa ca nibbānassa aikaṃsatau anulaumīti| tatau aiva apac‍canīkā, anudhammabhūtā ca, tassā ca paṭipan‍nattā ariyasaṅghau ‘‘suppaṭipan‍nau’’ti vuttauti dassaituṃ ‘‘suṭṭhu paṭipan‍nau’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tattha ‘‘anivattipaṭipada’’nti iminā ujuppaṭipattiṃ dassaiti| punappunaṃ nivattanai hi sati ujuppaṭipatti na hauti| ‘‘anulaumapaṭipadaṃ apac‍canīkapaṭipada’’nti  iminā ñāyappaṭipattiṃ| paṭipaj‍jitabbassa hi nibbānassa anulaumanaina, apac‍canīkatāya cassā ñāyatau| ‘‘dhammānudhammapaṭipada’’nti iminā sāmīcippaṭipattiṃ anucchavikabhāvadīpanatau| aitaina paṭhamapadassa papañ‍caniddaisau, itarāni tīṇi padānīti dassaiti| taināha ‘‘yasmā panā’’tiādi|

yathānusiṭṭhaṃ paṭipaj‍janaina kic‍casiddhitau ariyabhāvāvahaṃ savanaṃ sak‍kac‍casavanaṃ nāmāti vuttaṃ ‘‘sak‍kac‍caṃ suṇantīti sāvakā’’ti, taina ariyā aiva nippariyāyatau satthu sāvakā nāmāti dassaiti| sīladiṭṭhisāmañ‍ñatāyāti ariyaina sīlaina, ariyāya ca diṭṭhiyā samānabhāvaina| ariyānañhi sīladiṭṭhiyau majjhai bhin‍nasuvaṇṇaṃ viya nin‍nānākaraṇaṃ maggaināgatattā| taina  tai yattha katthaci ṭhitāpi saṃhatāva| taināha ‘‘saṅghātabhāvamāpan‍nau’’ti| māyāsāṭhaiyyādipāpadhammasamucchaidaina uju| tatau aiva gaumuttavaṅkābhāvaina avaṅkā| candalaikhāvaṅkābhāvaina akuṭilā| naṅgalakauṭivaṅkābhāvaina ajimhā| avaṅkādibhāvaina vā uju| parisuddhaṭṭhaina ariyā| apaṇṇakabhāvaina ñāyati kamati nibbānaṃ, taṃ vā ñāyati paṭivijjhīyati aitaināti ñāyau| vaṭṭadukkhaniyyānāya anucchavikattā| anurūpattā sāmīci aupāyikātipi saṅkhaṃ samañ‍ñaṃ gatā sammāpaṭipatti, tāya samaṅgitāya suppaṭipan‍nā ‘‘sammā paṭipaj‍jantī’’ti katvā| vattamānatthau hi ayaṃ paṭipan‍na-saddau yathā ‘‘sautāpattiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipan‍nau’’ti (pu॰ pa॰ 206)| atītaṃ paṭipadanti yathāvuttamaggasammāpaṭipattiṃ vadati| ubhayaina ca sāmañ‍ñaniddaisaina, ‘‘suppaṭipan‍nā ca suppaṭipan‍nā ca suppaṭipan‍nā’’ti aikasaisanayaina vā gahitānaṃ samūhau ‘‘suppaṭipan‍nau’’ti vuttauti dassaiti|

aivaṃ ādipadatthaniddaisabhāvaina itarapadānaṃ atthaṃ vatvā idāni catun‍nampi padānaṃ avaumissakaṃ atthaṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘apicā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tattha yathānusiṭṭhanti satthārā yathā anusiṭṭhaṃ, tathā anusāsanīanurūpanti atthau| apaṇṇakapaṭipadanti avirajjhanakapaṭipadaṃ, anavaj‍japaṭipattinti atthau| aittha ca svākkhātai dhammavinayai apaṇṇakapaṭipadaṃ paṭipan‍nattāti aivaṃ sambandhitabbaṃ| purimapadaina sambandhai dutiyapadaṃ na vattabbaṃ siyā, nanu ca dutiyapadaina sambandhaipi paṭhamaṃ padaṃ na vattabbaṃ siyāti? na tassa paṭhamaṃ apaikkhitattā| sā pana sāvakānaṃ apaṇṇakapaṭipadā yathānusiṭṭhaṃ paṭipadāti dassanatthaṃ ‘‘yathānusiṭṭhaṃ paṭipan‍nattā’’ti vattabbaṃ| ubhayassāpi vā suppaṭipan‍nabhāvasādhanattā ubhayaṃ vuttaṃ| tathā hi pi-saddaina ubhayaṃ samuc‍cinauti|

kilaisajimhavasaina antadvayagāhauti sabbasau taṃ pahāya sammāpaṭipadā kāyādivaṅkappahāyinī ujuppaṭipatti hautīti āha ‘‘majjhimāya paṭipadāya…pai॰… ujuppaṭipan‍nau’’ti|

ñāyau nāma yuttappattapaṭipatti, nibbānañ‍ca, sabbasaṅkhārasamathatāya ādittaṃ cailaṃ, sīsaṃ vā ajjhupaikkhitvāpi paṭipaj‍jitabbamaivāti āha ‘‘ñāyau vuc‍cati nibbāna’’nti| nibbāyanakiriyāmukhaina caittha nibbānaṃ vuttanti daṭṭhabbaṃ, maggañāṇādīhi vā ñāyati paṭivijjhīyati sacchikarīyati cāti ñāyau nibbānanti aivamaittha atthau daṭṭhabbau|

guṇasambhāvanāya paraihi kayiramānaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhānādisāmīcikammaṃ arahantīti sāmīcikammārahā|

156. yadidanti antaugadhaliṅgavacanabhaidau nipātauti tassa vacanabhaidaina atthamāha ‘‘yā imānī’’ti| aivanti pakāratthai nipātau, iminā pakāraināti atthau| taina itarāni tīṇi yugaḷāni dassitāni hauntīti āha ‘‘aivaṃ cattāri purisayugaḷāni hauntī’’ti| aitanti aitaṃ ‘‘purisapuggalā’’ti bahuvacanavasaina vuttaṃ padaṃ| purisā ca tai puggalā ca purisapuggalā| tattha ‘‘purisā’’ti iminā paṭhamāya pakatiyā gahaṇaṃ, ‘‘puggalā’’ti pana dutiyāyapi sattasāmañ‍ñaināti aivaṃ vā aittha atthau daṭṭhabbau| catun‍naṃ pac‍cayānaṃ kīdisānaṃ? ānaitvā hunitabbānanti adhikāratau pākaṭauyamatthau| dātabbanti vā pac‍cattavacanaṃ ‘‘catun‍naṃ pac‍cayāna’’nti padaṃ apaikkhitvā sāmivasaina pariṇāmaitabbaṃ, añ‍ñathā yaisaṃ kaisañ‍ci catun‍naṃ pac‍cayānaṃ ‘‘āhuna’’nti samañ‍ñā siyā| āhunaṃ arahatīti vā āhunaiyyau| sak‍kādīnampi vā āhavananti sak‍kādīhipi diyyamānaṃ dānaṃ| yattha hutaṃ mahapphalanti yasmiṃ āvahanīyaggimhi hutaṃ dadhiādi āhunaiyyaggigahapataggidakkhiṇaiyyaggīsu hutatau uḷāraphalanti taisaṃ brāhmaṇānaṃ laddhi| hutanti din‍naṃ| nikāyantaraiti sabbatthikavādinikāyai|

ṭhapaitvā 165 taiti tai piyamanāpai ñātimittai apanaitvā, taisaṃ adatvāti adhippāyau| aisa aisau| aikabuddhantarai ca dissatīti aikasmiṃ buddhantarai vītivattai dissati| ca-saddaina kadāci asaṅkhyaiyyaipi kappai vītivattaiti dassaiti| abbaukiṇṇanti paṭipakkhaihi avaumissaṃ, kiriyāvisaisakañ‍caitaṃ| piyamanāpattakaradhammā nāma sīlādayau, tai ariyasaṅghai suppatiṭṭhitā| ayaṃ haittha adhippāyau – ñātimittā vippayuttā na cirassaiva samāgacchanti, anavaṭṭhitā ca taisu piyamanāpatā, na aivamariyasaṅghau| tasmā saṅghauva pāhunaiyyauti| pubbakāranti aggakiriyaṃ| sabbappakāraināti ādaragāravabahumānādinā, daiyyadhammassa sak‍kac‍cakaraṇādinā  ca sabbaina pakāraina| svāyaṃ pāhavanīya-saddau ‘‘pāhunaiyyau’’ti vuc‍cati pariyāyabhāvaina|

dakkhanti aitāya sattā yathādhippaitāhi sampattīhi vaḍḍhantīti dakkhiṇā| tathābhāvakaraṇaina dakkhiṇaṃ arahati| yathā uḷārātivipuludrayalābhaina visaudhitaṃ nāma hauti, aivaṃ dakkhiṇā vipulaphalatāyāti vuttaṃ ‘‘mahapphalakaraṇatāya visaudhaitī’’ti|

puñ‍ñatthikaihi añ‍jali karaṇīyā aitthāti añ‍jalikaraṇīyau|

yadipi pāḷiyaṃ ‘‘anuttara’’nti vuttaṃ| natthi itau uttaraṃ visiṭṭhanti hi anuttaraṃ| samampissa pana natthīti dassaintau ‘‘asadisa’’nti āha| khittaṃ vuttaṃ bījaṃ mahapphalabhāvakaraṇaina tāyati rakkhati, khipanti vapanti aittha bījānīti vā khaittaṃ, kaidārādi, khaittaṃ viya khaittaṃ, puñ‍ñānaṃ khaittaṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ| saisaṃ buddhānussatiyaṃ vuttanayānusāraina vaiditabbaṃ|

4. sīlānussatikathāvaṇṇanā

158. ahau vata sīlāni akhaṇḍāni, ahau vata acchiddānīti aivaṃ sabbattha yaujaitabbaṃ| ahau vatāti ca sambhāvanai nipātau| taināha ‘‘akhaṇḍatādiguṇavasainā’’ti| akhaṇḍabhāvādisampattivasaināti atthau| parassa sīlāni anussariyamānāni piyamanāpabhāvāvahanaina kaivalaṃ maittāya padaṭṭhānaṃ haunti, na visuṃ kammaṭṭhānanti āha ‘‘attanau sīlāni anussaritabbānī’’ti| cāgādīsupi aisaiva nayau|

yaisanti  sīlādīnaṃ| paṭipāṭiyā samādānai samādānak‍kamaina, aikajjhaṃ samādānai uddaisak‍kamaina sīlānaṃ ādiantaṃ vaiditabbaṃ| pariyantai chin‍nasāṭakau viyāti vatthantai, dasantai vā chin‍navatthaṃ viya, visadisūdāharaṇaṃ caitaṃ| aivaṃ saisānipi udāharaṇāni| vinivaidhavasaina chin‍nasāṭakau vinividdhasāṭakau| visabhāgavaṇṇaina gāvī viyāti sambandhau| sabalarahitāni vā asabalāni| tathā akammāsāni| sattavidhamaithunasaṃyaugau haiṭṭhā sīlakathāyaṃ vuttau aiva| kaudhūpanāhādīhīti ādi-saddaina makkhapaḷāsaissāmacchariyamāyāsāṭhaiyyamānātimānādayau gahitā| tāniyaiva akhaṇḍādiguṇāni sīlāni| sīlassa taṇhādāsabyatau  maucanaṃ vivaṭṭūpanissayabhāvāpādanaṃ| tatau aiva taṃsamaṅgīpuggalau sairī sayaṃvasī bhujissau nāma hauti| taināha ‘‘bhujissabhāvakaraṇaina bhujissānī’’ti| ‘‘imināhaṃ sīlaina daivau vā bhavaiyyaṃ daivañ‍ñatarau vā, tattha nic‍cau dhuvau sassatau’’ti, ‘‘sīlaina suddhī’’ti ca aivamādinā taṇhādiṭṭhīhi aparāmaṭṭhattā ayaṃ tai sīlaisu dausauti catūsu vipattīsu yāya kāyaci vipattiyā dassanaina parāmaṭṭhuṃ anuddhaṃsaituṃ| samādhisaṃvattanappayaujanāni samādhisaṃvattanikāni|

159. sikkhāya sagāravauti sīladhanaṃ nissāya paṭiladdhasamādānattā sātisayaṃ sikkhāya sagāravau sappatissau hauti| sabhāgavuttīti sampan‍nasīlaihi sabhāgavuttikau, tāya aiva vā sikkhāya sabhāgavutti| yau hi sikkhāgāravarahitau, sau tāya visabhāgavutti nāma hauti vilaumanatau| yathā paraihi saddhiṃ attanau chiddaṃ na hauti, aivaṃ dhammāmisaihi paṭisantharaṇaṃ paṭisanthārau| sikkhāya sagāravattā aiva tattha appamattau hauti| attānuvādabhayaṃ parānuvādabhayaṃ daṇḍabhayaṃ duggatibhayanti aivamādīni bhayāni imassa dūrasamussāritānīti āha ‘‘attānuvādādibhayavirahitau’’ti| saisaṃ suviñ‍ñaiyyamaiva|

5. cāgānussatikathāvaṇṇanā

160. pakatiyāti sabhāvaina| cāgādhimuttaināti daiyyadhammaparic‍cāgai yuttappayuttaina tan‍nin‍naina tappauṇaina| nic‍caṃ sadā pavattā dānasaṃvibhāgā yassa sau nic‍capavattadānasaṃvibhāgau, taina| idhāpi ‘‘pakatiyā’’ti ānaitvā sambandhitabbaṃ| yaṃ parassa paṭiyattaṃ diyyati, taṃ dānaṃ| yaṃ attanā paribhuñ‍jitabbatau saṃvibhajati, sau saṃvibhāgau| itau dāni pabhutīti itau paṭṭhāya dāni aj‍jataggai aj‍jadivasaṃ ādiṃ katvā| adatvā na bhuñ‍jissāmīti dānasamādānaṃ katvā| taṃdivasanti tasmiṃ bhāvanārambhadivasai| tattha nimittaṃ gaṇhitvāti tasmiṃ dānai paric‍cāgacaitanāya pavattiākārassa sal‍lakkhaṇavasaina nimittaṃ gahaitvā| vigatamalamacchairatādiguṇavasaināti vigatāni malamacchairāni aitasmāti vigatamalamacchairau, cāgau, tassa bhāvau vigatamalamacchairatā| tadādīnaṃ guṇānaṃ, sampattīnaṃ, ānisaṃsānaṃ vā vasaina|

sataṃ  dhammaṃ anuk‍kamanti sādhūnaṃ baudhisattānaṃ dhammaṃ pavaiṇiṃ anu anu kamantau auk‍kamantau, avauk‍kamantau vā| yai imai dāyakassa lābhā āyuvaṇṇasukhabalapaṭibhānādayau, piyabhāvādayau ca bhagavatā saṃvaṇṇitā pakittitā| manussattaṃ vāti vā-saddau avuttavikappatthau, taina indriyapāṭavabhāvakammassakatāñāṇādīnaṃ saṅgahau daṭṭhabbau|

macchairamalaināti macchairasaṅkhātaina malaina| atha vā macchairañ‍ca malañ‍ca macchairamalaṃ, taina macchairaina caiva laubhādimalaina cāti atthau| pajāyanavasaināti yathāsakaṃ kammanibbattanavasaina| kaṇhadhammānanti laubhādiaikantakāḷakānaṃ pāpadhammānaṃ| vigatattāti pahīnattā| vigatamalamacchairaina caitasāti itthambhūtalakkhaṇai karaṇavacanaṃ| sautāpan‍nassa satauti gaihaṃ āvasantassa sautāpan‍nassa samānassa| nissayavihāranti nissāya viharitabbavihāraṃ, daivasikaṃ vaḷañ‍janakakammaṭṭhānanti atthau| abhibhavitvāti agāraṃ āvasantānaṃ añ‍ñaisaṃ uppaj‍janakarāgādiupak‍kilaisai abhibhuyya|

yau kiñ‍ci daintaupi sāpaikkhauva daiti, sau muttacāgau na hauti, ayaṃ pana na aivanti ‘‘muttacāgau’’ti vuttaṃ| vissaṭṭhacāgauti nirapaikkhaparic‍cāgauti atthau| yathā pāṇātipātabahulau puggalau ‘‘lauhitapāṇī’’ti vuc‍cati, aivaṃ dānabahulau puggalau ‘‘payatapāṇī’’ti vuttauti dassaintau ‘‘dātuṃ sadā dhautahatthauyaivā’’ti āha| tattha sadāti nic‍caṃ, abhiṇhanti atthau| paric‍cāgauti daiyyadhammaparic‍cāgauti adhikāratau viñ‍ñāyati| yaṃ yaṃ daiyyadhammaṃ parai yācanakā| yācanai yaugau yācanayaugau, paraihi yācituṃ yuttauti atthau| yājaina yuttauti dānaina yuttau sadā paric‍cajanatau| ubhayaiti yathāvuttai dānai, saṃvibhāgai ca ratau abhiratau|

161. yathā parisuddhasīlassa puggalassa akhaṇḍatādiguṇavasaina attanau sīlassa anussarantassa sīlānussatibhāvanā ijjhati, aivaṃ parisuddhauḷāraparic‍cāgassa puggalassa anupak‍kiliṭṭhamaiva  attanau paric‍cāgaṃ anussarantassa bhāvanā ijjhatīti dassaituṃ ‘‘vigatamalamacchairatādiguṇavasainā’’tiādi vuttaṃ, taṃ vuttatthamaiva|

bhiyyausauti  uparipi| mattāya pamāṇassa mahatiyā mattāya, vipulaina pamāṇaināti atthau| cāgādhimuttauti paric‍cāgai adhimuttau nin‍napauṇapabbhārau| tatau aiva alaubhajjhāsayau katthacipi anabhisaṅgacittau| paṭiggāhakaisu maittāyanavasaina paric‍cāgau hautīti maittāya maittābhāvanāya anulaumakārī anurūpapaṭipatti| visāradauti visadau attanauva guṇaina katthacipi amaṅkubhūtau abhibhuyya vihārī| saisaṃ suviñ‍ñaiyyamaiva|

6. daivatānussatikathāvaṇṇanā

162. ariyamaggavasaināti ariyamaggassa adhigamavasaina| samudāgataihīti sammadaiva taduppattitau uddhaṃ āgataihi| yādisā hi ariyānaṃ santānai laukiyāpi saddhādayau, na tādisā kadācipi pauthuj‍janikā saddhādayau| dibbantīti daivā| cattārau mahārājānau aitaisanti catumahārājā, tai aiva cātumahārājikā| taittiṃsa sahapuñ‍ñakārinau tatthūpapan‍nāti taṃsahacaritaṃ ṭhānaṃ tāvatiṃsaṃ, tan‍nivāsinaupi daivā taṃsahacaraṇatau aiva tāvatiṃsā| dukkhatau yātā apayātāti yāmā| tusāya pītiyā itā upagatāti tusitā| bhaugānaṃ nimmānai rati aitaisanti nimmānaratinau| paranimmitaisu bhaugaisu vasaṃ vattaintīti paranimmitavasavattinau| brahmānaṃ kāyau samūhau brahmakāyau, tappariyāpan‍natāya tattha bhavāti brahmakāyikā| tatuttarīti tatau brahmakāyikaihi uttari, upari parittābhādikai sandhāya vadati| daivatā sakkhiṭṭhānai ṭhapaitvāti ‘‘yathārūpāya maggaināgatāya saddhāya saman‍nāgatā, sīlaina sutaina cāgaina pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatā itau cutā tattha upapan‍nā tā daivatā, mayhampi tathārūpā saddhā sīlaṃ sutaṃ cāgau pañ‍ñā ca saṃvij‍jatī’’ti daivatā sakkhiṭṭhānai ṭhapaitvā sakkhiṃ autāraintaina viya attanau saddhādiguṇā anussaritabbā|

yadi aivaṃ suttai (a॰ ni॰ 6.10) ubhayaguṇānussaraṇaṃ vuttaṃ, taṃ kathanti anuyaugaṃ manasi katvā āha ‘‘suttai panā’’tiādi| tattha kiñ‍cāpi vuttanti ‘‘attanau ca, tāsañ‍ca daivatāna’’nti ubhayaṃ samadhuraṃ viya suttai (a॰ ni॰ 6.10) kiñ‍cāpi vuttaṃ| atha  khau taṃ ‘‘daivatānaṃ saddhañ‍ca sīlañ‍cā’’tiādivacanaṃ| sakkhiṭṭhānai ṭhapaitabbanti ‘‘vutta’’nti paratau padaṃ  ānaitvā sambandhitabbaṃ| kimatthaṃ pana sakkhiṭṭhānai ṭhapananti āha ‘‘daivatānaṃ attanau saddhādīhi samānaguṇadīpanatthanti vaiditabba’’nti| kasmā pana suttai yathārutavasaina atthaṃ aggahaitvā aivaṃ atthau gayhatīti āha ‘‘aṭṭhakathāyañhī’’tiādi|

163. tasmāti yasmā aṭṭhakathāyaṃ daivatā sakkhiṭṭhānai ṭhapaitvā attanau guṇānussaraṇaṃ daḷhaṃ katvā vuttaṃ, sīlacāgānussatīsu viya idhāpi attanau guṇānussaraṇaṃ jhānuppattinimittaṃ yuttaṃ, tasmā| pubbabhāgai bhāvanārambhai| aparabhāgaiti yathā bhāvaintassa upacārajjhānaṃ ijjhati, tathā bhāvanākālai| yadi attanau aiva idha guṇā anussaritabbā, kathamayaṃ daivatānussatīti āha ‘‘daivatānaṃ guṇasadisasaddhādiguṇānussaraṇavasainā’’ti| taina sadisakappanāya ayaṃ bhāvanā ‘‘daivatānussatī’’ti vuttā, na daivatānaṃ, tāsaṃ guṇānaṃ vā anussaraṇaināti dassaiti| pubbabhāgai vā pavattaṃ daivatāguṇānussaraṇaṃ upādāya ‘‘daivatānussatī’’ti imissā samañ‍ñā vaiditabbā| tathā hi vakkhati ‘‘pubbabhāgai daivatā ārabbha pavattacittavasainā’’ti| saisaṃ suviñ‍ñaiyyamaiva|

pakiṇṇakakathāvaṇṇanā

164. aitāsanti aitāsaṃ buddhānussatiādīnaṃ chan‍naṃ anussatīnaṃ| vitthāradaisanāyanti vitthāravasaina pavattadaisanāyaṃ, mahānāmasuttaṃ (a॰ ni॰ 6.10; 11.11) sandhāya vadati| bhagavātiādīnaṃ atthanti bhagavātiādīnaṃ padānaṃ atthaṃ| atthavaidanti vā haituphalaṃ paṭic‍ca uppan‍naṃ tuṭṭhimāha| dhammavaidanti haituṃ paṭic‍ca uppan‍naṃ tuṭṭhiṃ| ‘‘ārakattā araha’’nti anussarantassa hi yaṃ taṃ bhagavatau kilaisaihi ārakattaṃ, sau haitu| ñāpakau caittha haitu adhippaitau, na kārakau, sampāpakau vā| yaunaina ñāyamānau arahattatthau, taṃ phalaṃ| iminā nayaina saisapadaisupi haituphalavibhāgau vaiditabbau| dhammānussatiādīsupi ‘‘ādimajjhapariyausānakalyāṇattā’’tiādinā, ‘‘yasmā pana sā sammāpaṭipadā’’tiādinā ca tattha tattha haituapadaisau katauyaivāti| dhammūpasaṃhitanti yathāvuttahaituhaituphalasaṅkhātaguṇūpasaṃhitaṃ| guṇaiti attanau guṇai|

165. ariyasāvakānañ‍ñaiva  ijjhantīti ariyasāvakānaṃ ijjhantiyaivāti uttarapadāvadhāraṇaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, avadhāraṇañ‍ca taisaṃ sukhasiddhidassanatthaṃ| taināha ‘‘taisaṃ hī’’tiādi| na puthuj‍janānaṃ sabbaina sabbaṃ ijjhantīti| tathā hi vakkhati ‘‘aivaṃ santaipī’’tiādi| yadi aivaṃ, kasmā mahānāmasuttādīsu (a॰ ni॰ 6.10; 11.11) bahūsu suttaisu ariyasāvakaggahaṇaṃ katanti? taisaṃ bahulavihāratāyāti ācariyā|

nikkhantanti niggataṃ nissaṭaṃ| muttanti vissaṭṭhaṃ| vuṭṭhitanti apaitaṃ| sabbamaitaṃ vikkhambhanamaiva sandhāya vadati| gaidhamhāti palibaudhatau| idampīti buddhānussativasaina laddhaṃ upacārajjhānamāha| ārammaṇaṃ karitvāti pac‍cayaṃ katvā, pādakaṃ katvāti atthau| visujjhantīti paramatthavisuddhiṃ pāpuṇanti|

sambādhaiti taṇhāsaṃkilaisādinā sampīḷai saṃkaṭai gharāvāsai| aukāsādhigamau laukuttaradhammassa adhigamāya adhigantabbaaukāsau| anussatiyau aiva anussatiṭṭhānāni| visuddhidhammāti visujjhanasabhāvā, visujjhituṃ vā bhabbā| paramatthavisuddhidhammatāyāti paramatthavisuddhiyā nibbānassa bhabbabhāvaina| upak‍kamaināti payaugaina| pariyaudapanāti visaudhanā|

166. aivaṃ santaipīti aivaṃ mahānāmasuttādīsu anaikaisu suttaisu ariyasāvakassaiva vasaina chasu anussatīsu daisitāsupi| yassānubhāvaināti yassa cittappasādassa balaina| pītiṃ paṭilabhitvāti anussavavasaina buddhārammaṇaṃ pītiṃ uppādaitvā|

chaanussatiniddaisavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

iti sattamaparicchaidavaṇṇanā|

8. anussatikammaṭṭhānaniddaisavaṇṇanā

maraṇassatikathāvaṇṇanā

167. itauti  daivatānussatiyā| sā hi chasu anussatīsu sabbapacchā niddiṭṭhattā āsan‍nā, pac‍cakkhā ca| anantarāyāti tadanantaraṃ uddiṭṭhattā vuttaṃ| maraṇassa sati maraṇassatīti maraṇaṃ tāva dassaituṃ ‘‘tattha maraṇa’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| kāmañ‍caittha khandhānaṃ bhaidau ‘‘jarāmaraṇaṃ dvīhi khandhaihi saṅgahita’’nti (dhātu॰ 71) vacanatau cutikhandhānaṃ vināsau maraṇanti vattabbaṃ, visaisatau pana taṃ jīvitindriyassa vināsabhāvaina kammaṭṭhānikānaṃ pac‍cupatiṭṭhatīti ‘‘jīvitindriyassa upacchaidau’’ti vuttaṃ| tainaivāha niddaisai ‘‘kaḷaivarassa nikkhaipau jīvitindriyassupacchaidau’’ti (vibha॰ 236; dī॰ ni॰ 2.390; ma॰ ni॰ 1.92, 123)| tattha aikabhavapariyāpan‍nassāti aikaina bhavaina paricchin‍nassa| jīvitindriyassa upacchaidauti jīvitindriyappabandhassa vicchaidau āyukkhayādivasaina antaradhānaṃ| samucchaidamaraṇanti arahatau santānassa sabbasau ucchaidabhūtaṃ maraṇaṃ| saṅkhārānaṃ khaṇabhaṅgasaṅkhātanti saṅkhatadhammānaṃ udayavayaparicchin‍nassa pavattikhaṇassa bhaṅgau niraudhauti saṅkhaṃ gataṃ| khaṇikamaraṇanti yathāvuttakhaṇavantaṃ tasmiṃyaiva khaṇai labbhamānaṃ maraṇaṃ| yaṃ khandhappabandhaṃ upādāya rukkhādisamañ‍ñā, tasmiṃ anupacchin‍naipi al‍latādivigamanaṃ nissāya matavauhārau sammutimaraṇaṃ| na taṃ idha adhippaitanti taṃ samucchaidakhaṇikasammutimaraṇaṃ idha maraṇānussatiyaṃ nādhippaitaṃ abāhul‍latau, anupaṭṭhahanatau, asaṃvaigavatthutau cāti adhippāyau|

adhippaitaṃ satiyā ārammaṇabhāvaina| āyuādīnaṃ khayakālai maraṇaṃ kālamaraṇaṃ| taisaṃ aparikkhīṇakālai maraṇaṃ akālamaraṇaṃ| ‘‘kālamaraṇaṃ puñ‍ñakkhayainā’’ti idaṃ yathādhikāraṃ sampattibhavavasaina vuttaṃ, vipattibhavavasaina pana ‘‘pāpakkhayainā’’ti vattabbaṃ|

āyusantānajanakapac‍cayasampattiyāti  āyuppabandhassa pavattāpanakānaṃ āhārādipac‍cayānaṃ sampattiyaṃ| vipak‍kavipākattāti attanau phalānurūpaṃ dātabbavipākassa din‍nattā| gatikālāhārādisampattiyā abhāvaināti  daivānaṃ viya gatisampattiyā, paṭhamakappiyānaṃ viya kālasampattiyā, uttarakurukādīnaṃ viya āhārasampattiyā ca abhāvaina| ādi-saddaina utusuk‍kasauṇitādiṃ pariggayhati| aj‍jatanakālapurisānaṃ viyāti viya-saddau aṭṭhānai paṭṭhapitau, aj‍jatanakālapurisānaṃ vassasatamattaparimāṇassa viya āyunau khayavasaināti yaujanā| aivaṃ hi anāgataipi paramāyunau saṅgahau katau hauti, atītai pana gatisampattiyā viya kālāhārādisampattiyā bhāvaina anaikavassasahassa parimāṇaupi āyu ahausi| ayañ‍ca vibhāgau vattamānassa antarakappassa vasaina vuttauti daṭṭhabbaṃ| kalāburājādīnanti ādi-saddaina nandayakkhanandamāṇavakādīnaṃ saṅgahau daṭṭhabbau| taṃ sabbampīti yaṃ ‘‘kālamaraṇaṃ akālamaraṇaṃ, tatthāpi puñ‍ñakkhayamaraṇaṃ āyukkhayamaraṇaṃ ubhayakkhayamaraṇaṃ upak‍kamamaraṇa’’nti vuttappabhaidaṃ maraṇaṃ, taṃ sabbampi|

168. yaunisauti upāyaina| manasikārauti maraṇārammaṇau manasikārau| yaina pana upāyaina manasikārau pavattaitabbau, taṃ byatiraikamukhaina dassaituṃ ‘‘ayaunisau pavattayatau’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tattha ayaunisau pavattayatauti anupāyaina satiṃ, saṃvaigaṃ, ñāṇañ‍ca anupaṭṭhapaitvā maraṇaṃ anussaratau| tadaitaṃ sabbampīti saukapāmauj‍jānaṃ uppaj‍janaṃ, saṃvaigassa anuppaj‍janaṃ, santāsassa uppaj‍janañ‍ca yathāk‍kamaṃ satisaṃvaigañāṇaviharatau hauti| yau hi ‘‘maraṇaṃ nāmaitaṃ jātassa aikantika’’nti paṇḍitānaṃ vacanaṃ sarati, tattha saṃvaigajātau hauti sampajānau ca, tassa iṭṭhajanamaraṇādinimittaṃ saukādayau anaukāsāva| tasmāti yasmā ayaunisau manasikāraṃ pavattayatau aitai dausā, tasmā| tattha tatthāti arañ‍ñasusānādīsu| hatamatasattaiti caurādīhi hatai, sarasaina matai ca sattai| diṭṭhapubbā sampatti yaisaṃ tai diṭṭhapubbasampattī, taisaṃ diṭṭhapubbasampattīnaṃ| yaujaitvāti upaṭṭhapaitvā| aikac‍cassāti tikkhindriyassa ñāṇuttarassa|
